We have many departments at your Bhaktivedanta Manor. We pray and meditate how to refine our service and become experts in all respects. Devotional service for your pleasure is never static. We constantly need to access our internal motives and our service expertise. Any attempt to enjoy the fruits is quickly thwarted. Subtle enjoyment and calculations for self-glory appear and fade over the days, weeks and years.

You taught us how to see everything in relation to Kṛṣṇa, as well as to carefully look after all the facilities He gave us to use in His service. You saw to it that nothing was wasted, misused or abused. You left us so much wealth in your instructions; we must preserve and assimilate it. This transcendental legacy of devotional service must be passed on intact to the many generations to come.

As the years go by since your disappearance, it is important not to forget the simple lessons you taught. You were so concerned over what seemed a minor detail – the cleanliness of your temples, a tap slightly dripping water, a light left on in a room, a wasted *paisa,* a wasted moment, some small black burn mark in the bottom of a milk pot, the care of guests that came to your temple, the cultivation of congregation members, the systematic spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness through the *saṅkīrtana* movement, to have open, simple-hearted relationships with fellow devotees. The tasks you set us demand more and more surrender.

Certainly this will require us to look deeper into our hearts. Please give us the courage to make the changes required. We know that ultimately your will will prevail, and that gradually you will take us to the perfection of our service and our lives.

You will be pleased to hear that after much thought, consideration and consultation, we have revamped and began to put in place a new training program. We offer *Bhakti-śāstrī* degrees and soon a range of other doctrinal degrees will follow. Development of vocational training is planned.

We are learning new teaching, training and communication skills from friends and professionals and are now beginning to successfully use them in Kṛṣṇa’s service.

Your temple is becoming more famous than ever. On the 6thof March, under threat of prosecution, we were supposed to stop the public from coming to the temple for *darśana* and festivals. We wanted to stage a significant protest in London on that day, and to our amazement, 36 000 people turned out to show their support for the temple. What was so moving were the feelings of each one of them for your temple and what you had achieved. If we ever needed proof of how you have affected so many people’s lives, we had it. The temple was given a short reprieve and we are still waiting to find out what the future will hold. However, we have full confidence that you will be ever-increasingly prominent for the people of Britain and that Bhaktivedanta Manor will play an important role in that.

In one way or another according to the individual, you are teaching us to surrender. Please continue to accept our service, thus giving further opportunities for purification and advancement. Bring us all to the point of pure *bhakti,* that devotional sentiment that gives ecstatic pleasure to you, to all our predecessor *ācāryas*, to all the Lord’s confidential associates, to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī and to Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Please take us all back home, back to Godhead.

*From your aspiring servants of Bhaktivedanta Manor*.

## Birmingham

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

As your Vyāsa-pūjā day celebrations approach, we few devotees in Birmingham prepare to glorify you as far as our means and realizations allow. Yet despite our most determined efforts, the foregone conclusion is that we are unable to adequately glorify even the faint shadow of your greatness. Can a dwarf jump and touch the moon?

Even if the demigods were to pay homage to you with the choicest poetry and paraphernalia from now until the end of millennium, still they would fail to enumerate any significant portion of your transcendental glories. For who are you? You are the foremost of the gaura-priya-janas, the dear devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You are svarūpa-śakti-puṣṭa-parikāra, the embodiment of the benediction moon of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu which nourishes the spiritual life of the fallen souls in this Kali-yuga. It is stated that just as there is no purpose to this universe without the sun and the moon to sustain the life herein, similarly there is no purpose to this planet without pure devotees like yourself traversing it to give life to its spiritually emaciated souls.

*janasya kṛṣṇād vimukhasya daivād*  
*adharma-śīlasya suduḥkhitasya*  
*anugrahāyeha caranti nūnaṁ*  
*bhūtāni bhavyāni janārdanasya*

**”O my Lord, great philanthropic souls travel on the earth on behalf of the Supreme Personality of Godhead to show some compassion to the fallen souls who are averse to the sense of subordination to the Lord.” (*****Bhāg*****. 3.5.3)**

Such devotees are the ornaments of the earth, and of such ornaments you are the crown jewel, *śiromaṇi mahājana*. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have brought the treasure house of love of God from Vṛndāvana. Although you have physically departed, you are still showering your mercy upon us in so many ways. You have given us the shelter of *grantha-rāja Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* through the illumination of your purports.

*kṛṣṇa-tulya bhāgavata – vibhu, sarvāśraya*  
*prati-śloke prati-akṣare nānā artha kaya*

**”All should take shelter of this** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **which is as good as Kṛṣṇa Himself. Every verse and every letter expresses different meanings.” (*****Cc. Madhya*** **24.318)**

Most of all you have given us the shelter of your pure representatives who are indiscriminately distributing the treasure that you have bestowed upon them – pure love of God, Kṛṣṇa-*prema*.

*dui bhāgavata dvārā diyā bhakti-rasa,*  
*tāṅhāra hṛdaye tāṅra preme haya vaśa*

**”Through the actions of these two** ***bhagavatas (Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*** **and the pure devotee) the Lord instills the mellows of transcendental loving service into the heart of every living being, and thus the Lord in the heart of His devotee, comes under the control of the devotees’ love.” (*****Cc. Adi*** **1.100)**

Our misfortune however is that out of madness we are not accepting your gifts. We remain insanely attached to this cesspool of material existence, still salivating at the sight of stool when the opportunity arises for again, adoration or just pain sense gratification. In this way, our hearts are decorated with *dambho darpo ‘bhimānaś ca*, with pride, arrogance and deceit. (*Bg*. 16.4)

So how can we glorify you properly? Our situation is hopeless, and so, in this condition, we are running desperately to you for help. You are *adoṣa-darśī*, you do not see the numerous faults of others, and you are *patitoddhārā*, the deliverer of the fallen. As such, we are crying and throwing ourselves in the dust of your lotus feet. Please find a way to break our attachments. Please purify our hearts so that we can glorify you properly. Whatever it takes, please be merciful to us. If we do not receive your mercy in this way, our lives are not worth living.

*Begging to always remain the servants of your servants, ISKCON Birmingham, England*.  
(Written by Bhāgavata-dharma dāsa)

## Boise

Dera Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. You are our merciful grandfather. You left all the peacefulness of Vṛndāvana to lift us from this ocean of nescience.

We are so fallen that we do not know the ABC’s of spiritual life, such as cleanliness, chanting, minimum standard of Deity worship and compassion. We are repeatedly told by our congregation how unqualified we are to run the center. We have no ability to preach and our “holier than thou” attitude drove many away. We were discouraged, disheartened and cried. Our false ego told us we should close the center, but, like a merciful father, you encourage us on and give us the intelligence to correct our mistakes. Because you are patita-pāvana, savior of the most fallen, and we are so fallen we receive your special mercy. Every time we fall, you send your emissaries to pick us up.

We can see your hand in every step of the development of the Boise center. Things, such as Ratha-yātrās, preaching engagements and the establishment of Govinda’s restaurant were arranged almost automatically as if you were personally tending to it. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*Your unworthy servants at the Boise center, Bhaktivedanta Bhakti Yoga Center, Boise, Idaho.*

## Boston

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

It is a great privilege to be able to sincerely offer our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day. Indeed, simply by acknowledging your greatness, one can cross this vast ocean of worldly sufferings.

You emphatically declare to your disciples: “So you simply follow my instructions as I have given to you and surely your life will be a success.” (letter to Nṛhari, 11/74) Jaya Prabhupāda! If one can actually understand even a fraction of your magnanimity and mercy they will immediately agree to become your follower.

Once someone asked you if you could perform miracles as other saints have. At this you gestured to your disciples who were present, “this is my miracle.” With one wave of your magic cane you have trashed our conceptions of enjoyment in this world. Now we have dedicated ourselves to the performance of austerity for the pleasure of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. This phenomenon is described in your letter to Rāmarāya March ’69:

“There is a verse in *Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu* in which it is stated, the more you advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the more you become disgusted with the material modes of life. When the American boys are disgusted with cigarette smokers, or listening to rock music and meaningless chattering, it means there is Kṛṣṇa’s grace. May Kṛṣṇa bless you more and more as you advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”

Not long ago, we were all engaging in activities that were certain to drag us to hell. Now, by your grace, we are part of an entire society who’s members are directing their activities toward achieving the highest goal of life. This international society you have created is our saving grace, as you explained to Śacisuta in February of ’70: “The association of devotees is the most important element in learning Kṛṣṇa consciousness way of life. By association with undesirable companions we have learned so many bad habits and similarly by association with pure persons or devotees we can become purified of our acquired bad habits. So constant association and working cooperatively with devotees is the prime and easy method to re-learn our original good habit of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”

You are the founding father and the very core of ISCKON. We all look to you for unquestionably pure association and by cooperating together nicely we will reciprocate the love that you are giving us.

## Brisbane

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We are offering our most humble and respectful obeisances at the lotus feet of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda on this most auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā.

This day is so important not only for the disciples but for all of human society. It was on this day that the illusion of material existence was challenged by an empowered devotee of Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda’s presentation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness was so appropriate to the time, place, and circumstances that no sane person could ignore the logic of abandoning sinful activities and taking to the devotional service of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda was able to present the highest conclusions of the Vedic knowledge in such a practical and understandable manner that the cult of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu was firmly established beyond the shores of India. This feat was thought by others who had tried, to be impossible. It was an impossible task accomplished in a few short years and performed in such a splendid manner, giving joy and inspiration to hear of when we think of the power the material energy has for keeping the conditioned souls enthralled and unknowingly imprisoned in pursuit of useless goals. When we think that our beloved spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, was able to lift this veil of *māyā* and give us a glimpse of reality, we are overcome with appreciation.

As followers of Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have been extracted from the general masses engaged in materialistic activities and graphically shown the real goal of life. The task of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness from our small, but dynamic Śrī Śrī Gaura Nitāi temple in Brisbane, is that goal of life. We cry out in all humility for the blessings of Śrīla Prabhupāda so that we can continue our service and not be swayed by the influence of *māyā*.

*Your most insignificant servants at ISKCON Brisbane Yātrā, Australia*.

## Canberra

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet. Please give us the strength to carry on in devotional service. Kṛṣṇa is yours, and you have the power to give Him to us, for you are His most confidential servant and an eternally liberated person.

The process to gain the benediction of the spiritual master is clearly outlined in the Śrī Śrī Gurv-aṣṭaka by Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura. Verse eight declares: “By the mercy of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day I should offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master.”

We, the devotees in Canberra, are trying to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness; by your causeless mercy, we have two Food for Life programs a week where we distribute *prasādam* to homeless young people or anyone in need. We have a vegetarian club at the Australian National University once a week plus the Sunday program. Our congregation is increasing, a couple of new boys have come to take shelter at your lotus feet, and we pray that you give us the strength to continue and increase our preaching efforts so that before your Centennial we can offer you at least the starting of a temple here in Canberra.

*From your fallen devotees at Canberra Temple, Australia*.

## Chapel Hill

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

I never really know what to say to you in gratitude or how to properly glorify you on Vyāsa-pūjā day. And since I am writing on behalf of my Godbrothers and Godsisters, I find that I have a tendency to show off my erudition in a forum of this type. But, Śrīla Prabhupāda, I do not want to pollute this offering of thanks with my own impurity. Rather, I want to speak for all of us associated with the project in Chapel Hill and sincerely make this offering of gratitude and glorification.

1\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the *jagat-guru* who has descended in this dark Twentieth Century to give the light of Caitanya Mahāprabhu to fallen hedonists like us.

2\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the one unifying personality to whom we all can bow down and beg for Kṛṣṇa’s mercy. Whatever we have done, are now doing, or will do in the future in the service of the Lord is done by your mercy alone.

3\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, your glories are never to be fully understood by our imperfect senses or limited mental strength. Both the Supreme Personality of Godhead and the Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead are absolute and thus beyond the limits of conditioned souls to fully understand.

4\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, your teachings are the only hope for peace and progress in this rapidly deteriorating world, where so-called civilized people daily kill each other in the name of bodily designations such as religion, nationality, and ethnic identity.

5\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your empowerment we are able to preach effectively to all classes of people (even the most highly educated and philosophical) and defeat their atheistic or impersonalistic ideas.

6\) Śrīla Prabhupāda, by Your Divine Grace only will we be delivered from *māyā’s* grip and once more fully realize our loving relationship with Kṛṣṇa. Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we offer our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet and beg for just one drop from the ocean of your unlimited mercy.

*Your aspiring servants in Chapel Hill, North Carolina*  
(Written by Dṛṣṭa Prabhu)

## Chicago

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of those lotus feet, which are the perfection that fulfills all desires. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this ninety-eight anniversary of your appearance in this otherwise meaningless world.

In just sixteen months the year of your Centennial Celebration will be upon us. Preparations are being made throughout the world by individuals, temples, and ISKCON as a whole to make your Centennial Celebration very grand in order to spread your fame among the general mass of people. Here in Chicago we are faced with the challenge of educating some seven million people about your movement. But as you can see, we are relatively low on men and money. Thus to meet this challenge we will have to rely on spiritual strength and assets rather than material ones.

However, as we take our inventory, we must admit that we have not made considerable spiritual advancement and have remained as insignificant stars instead of becoming the bright full moons you have requested us to be. Nor can we boast that we have any significant achievements to describe to you today. On the contrary, as we approach your Centennial year we can’t help but be ashamed that we are far from the goal of making your fame known in every home and to each individual. In fact we are bewildered as to how to proceed to accomplish this task.

You have mentioned that everyone is searching for Kṛṣṇa. We have experienced, however, that our three-minute and even three-hour speeches are so ineffectual that our interactions with others tend to have them in their sleeping state, unlike your intercourses with others, which miraculously revived the dormant Kṛṣṇa consciousness of even the lowest of men. Surely if we had a drop of your potency, our preaching would be unlimitedly successful and we would be able to offer you in 1996 a long list of impressive preaching accomplishments.

You have written (*Bhāg*. 4.20.25): “The transcendental vibration from the mouth of a pure devotee is so powerful that it can revive the living entity’s memory of his eternal relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead... Although when a pure devotee speaks, the articulation of his voice may resemble the sound of this material sky, the voice is spiritually very powerful because it touches the particles of saffron dust on the lotus feet of the Lord. As soon as a sleeping living entity hears the powerful voice emanating from the mouth of a pure devotee, he immediately remembers his eternal relationship with the Lord, although up until that moment he had forgotten everything.”

It can be concluded, therefore, that our lack of successful preaching must be due to our not properly delivering your spiritually powerful words to the sleeping living entities. This in turn must be due to our lack of faith in the potency of your words and our subsequent failure to associate with them with rapt attention and willful submission.

If we lack faith in the transcendental potency of your words, we will simply exist as “apathetic monks, growing old, holding on to religious principles and rituals, devoid of life.” As a result, our preparations for your Centennial Celebration will consist of ineffective hype and propaganda culminating in a pretentious and merely external festival of fruits and flowers. On the other hand, if we have unflinching faith in the spiritual power of your voice, we will distribute your words with a sense of urgency to the conditioned souls whom you are so compassionately concerned about. Then upon receiving your association in the form of your *vāṇī,* all things, which are troublesome to their hearts, will immediately be destroyed and loving service to the Supreme Lord will be established in their hearts as an irrevocable fact. In this way you will quickly become the most famous personality to whom all others will wish to surrender.

Therefore, the most important propaganda work we need to perform to broadcast your fame throughout the world is to purely deliver your words to others. We will not be able to do this though if our impure consciousness keeps us from desiring to please you in this way. Thus we daily pray that “our only wish is to have our consciousness purified by the words emanating form your lotus mouth,” for your words are the only medicine that can awaken us from our deliberate slumber and convince us to abandon our causeless unwillingness to share your mercy with others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow upon this one benediction: that we will have unflinching faith in and will engage in uninterrupted service to your words. Without this our love for you will merely be a sentimental, psychological phenomenon and your nectarean association will remain hidden from the ever-thirsty populace.

*Aspiring to become your instruments,*  
Your servants in Chicago  
(Written by Dānakeli dāsī)

## Copenhagen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your divine lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your appearance and on every other day of the year. Your timely appearance on this earth planet has brought auspiciousness to all the fallen conditioned souls of this world.

Your gifts to us:

Most precious of gifts,  
Medicine for Kali-age,  
Name of Kṛṣṇa on our lips,  
Released from material cage,  
The key to pure love  
You gave,  
Bestower of the holy name,  
Your Divine Grace.

*Prasādam*,  
The perfect diet,  
Insatiable tongue  
Becomes quiet,  
Honoring Kṛṣṇa’s *prasādam*,  
ISKCON’s preaching ace,  
Benefactor of the world,  
Your Divine Grace.

When the night is dark,  
The souls are in slumber.  
Awake with your dictaphone,  
You gave the answers;  
Bhaktivedanta purports,  
Your ISKCON is based on  
For the next 10 000 years,  
Your Divine Grace.

Your temples are like oases  
Shelter for the poor,  
Embassies of Vaikuṇṭha,  
Visas to the spiritual world.  
Deliverer of the fallen,  
Creator of holy places,  
We take shelter of you,  
Your Divine Grace.

In Vṛndāvana the holiest of *dhāmas*  
A temple rose in Raman-reti.

You consider this your home;  
Life’s desired *param-gati*.  
By your mercy,  
Darshan of this holiest place,  
Eternally indebted to,  
Your Divine Grace.

In Māyāpur, Gaurāṅga’s land  
Divine love can be easily gained.  
You build a city  
To spread Gaurāṅga’s fame.  
As your master plan unfolds,  
Lakhs will come and be amazed.  
All glories to you,  
Your Divine Grace.

A tidal wave  
Of love of God,  
Inundation of the world  
You have wrought.  
Spiritual ecstasy  
We can taste,  
By your mercy,  
Your Divine Grace.

Kṛṣṇa is reality,  
You have shown.  
Engage in *bhakti*;  
He can be known  
In His form of Śyāmasundara  
Face to face.  
Eternally indebted to  
Your Divine Grace.

Impersonalism, materialism,  
Nationalism, communism,  
You exposed these as illusions  
And established Kṛṣṇaism.  
A house big enough,  
Shelter for the human race,  
Well-wisher of the world,  
Your Divine Grace.

A revolution in consciousness  
You have begun.  
So short a time,  
Yet so much done.  
Dynamic, fearless and bold,  
You set the pace  
For the generations to come,  
Your Divine Grace.

What you have given  
Is meant to be shared,  
To introduce to the world  
An association so rare.  
Through your Centennial,  
Unity of the human race  
To eternally glorify you,  
Your Divine Grace.

Oh bestower of  
Pure devotion,  
Free us from  
Material motivations;  
Eternally in Kṛṣṇa’s service;  
No more rat race.

Bow down to you,  
Your Divine Grace.

*Begging to remain your servants at New Śrīvāsa Aṅgana, Copenhagen, Denmark*.  
(by Cīrañjīva dāsa and Rādhā-vinoda dāsa)

## Coventry

Due to your limitless mercy, so much has happened in Coventry since your last Vyāsa-pūjā that we don’t know where to start.

For the devotees here, for many years, just to come together on a Sunday at someone’s house, usually Viṣṇu Prīyā’s, and congregationally chant, dance, discuss from your beautiful *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* and partake in Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* was the highlight of the week.

As the numbers increased alongside our enthusiasm, you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, via H.H. Kṛṣṇa dāsa Mahārāja gave us mere fledglings a building. After years of hard work by the eager congregation the building evolved into a beautiful temple and has now become the pride and joy of our lives.

H.H. Kṛṣṇa dāsa Mahārāj, knowing your desire, encouraged us to perform evening *ārati* each day of the week. Once this was achieved, His Holiness asked us to perform the morning *ārati* also. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will be pleased to know that this daily worship is continuing with great vigor.

Every sincere step yields good results! We think that as a result of the above worship, on the 1stof April, 1994, a set of most beautiful Gaura-Nitāi Deities arrived majestically in Coventry all the way from Israel. This transcendental arrival came about through the power of prayer! Haridāsa from Coventry, during the period of the Gaura-purnimā Festival on the 11thof March, 1994, said a personal prayer at the Yogapīṭha to Śrī Jagannātha Miśra and Śrīmatī Śacīmātā to kindly give birth to Nimāi in his heart to make his life complete. Three hours later Haridāsa met an Israeli devotee called Iśvara Kṛṣṇa dāsa who had also said a deep prayer to his personal Gaura-Nitāi Deities to find a nice temple who would accept and worship another set of Deities that were donated to him but stayed resting in storage for the last five years. When your two grand disciples met by accident through a mutual friend of theirs (Govinda Bhāṣya), it turned into a joyous moment when they realized that their prayers were answered so soon. This is nothing short of a miracle. So Śrīla Prabhupāda, you can understand our mood now that the two Lordships have arrived in Coventry: suddenly, we feel that we are on the spiritual map as one of your conquests. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we surrender without struggle! Jaya *Jagat-guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda!

After the arrival of the Deities, the congregation’s mood is one of an expectant parent! Yes! We are all anxiously waiting for the installation day so we can look after Baby Śacīnandana and Nitāicānda by daily dressing Them up nicely, garlanding Them each morning with fresh flowers and singing Their glories and dancing in front of Them!

All this has happened because Lord Kṛṣṇa gave us Śrīla Prabhupāda! No one can equal you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave the whole world Vedic culture and a happy ten thousand years! The whole world of today and tomorrow bows at your lotus feet on this auspicious day.

Keep giving us your blessings! For us, in Coventry, in is only the beginning and yet it is so hard to contain our ecstasy. What will happen when you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us the lot? We wonder if we will be able to handle it!

*Happy Appearance Day, Śrīla Prabhupāda*!  
Coventry Yātrā, England

## Dallas

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our trying-to-be-humble obeisances. All glories to your service to His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī. All glories to the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who has made arrangements to send great pure devotees like yourself to deliver the fallen conditioned souls bound to the networks of material nature.

Lord Kṛṣṇa is the spiritual master of Arjuna. He instructed Arjuna to always think of Him, offer obeisances to Him, and to become His devotee. He further advised him to abandon all varieties of engagement and surrender unto Him. “Fear not,” the Supreme Lord said, “for I will protect you from all sinful reactions.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our spiritual master and our only way to understand transcendental life as it is, is to think of you, offer obeisances unto you, and to become your devotee. Yes! We must cease all engagements and surrender unto you. Just as the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa encouraged Arjuna to have no fear from any sinful reactions, your mercy is there to protect us. Arjuna protected his disciple Satyaki. While Satyaki was unconscious, Bhūriśrava attempted to kill him with his sword. As the downward stroke of the sword threatened Satyaki’s life, Arjuna drew an arrow and severed the arm of Bhūriśrava from behind. This action drew great protest from the soldiers of the battlefield who thought his deed was unchivalrous. Arjuna revealed that Satyaki was his disciple and that it was his duty to protect him. This explanation appeased the armies of Kurukṣetra removing their doubts. Dear sir, you are our Arjuna. If we depend on you and follow your instructions with faith, you are there to protect us from birth, death, old age, and disease.

Sir, Lord Kṛṣṇa disclosed that He is the swift deliverer of persons from the ocean of birth and death. We say, by taking the dust of your lotus feet, we mean sincerely taking shelter, and controlling our senses is the way to deliverance. You are the swift deliverer, just like the rain cloud that extinguishes the blazing forest fire. Not taking shelter of the bona fide spiritual master in disciplic succession degrades one to hellish existence. Only through surrender to a great personality such as Your Lordship can all the realities of spiritual activities, the Personality of Godhead and self-realization be revealed.

Lord Brahmā is the disciple of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. He, Lord Brahmā, very eloquently glorifies his spiritual master, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. We are very fallen, sir, and not very learned or self-realized. Somehow or other we’ve gatherer together our broken consciousness and tried to humbly employ our souls in your service. By Your Divine Grace we will continue to make advancement and sometime perhaps be expressive of your glories following in the footsteps of Lord Brahmā and other great *ācāryas*. You are most benevolent, and we are certain your glories are fathomless. Please let us continue to take steps toward a true understanding of your radiance.

How could anyone in the world receive transcendental knowledge concerning the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Kṛṣṇa unless an exceptionally compassionate, noble and exalted personality comparable to your kind and generous self desires to distribute the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu? Lord Caitanya and Lord Kṛṣṇa proclaimed the science of self-realization to very elevated persons, but you, sir, have sermonized this imperishable science of yoga to us, the most fallen rascals of Western civilization. You’ve injected real character, real humility, real fearlessness and real tolerance into our souls. Please remind us of our spiritual position if we become puffed-up and forgetful. You gave us good qualifications and great facilities to propagate Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. We pray that we may not forget that all the mercy is coming from you and that we surrender to your service.

Many volumes of books could be composed regarding your glorious attributes. You embrace our souls to the core. Indeed you are our life and soul. Thank you very much.

*Your eternal servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kālachāndjī Dhāma, Dallas, Texas*.  
(Written by Śrī Rāga dāsa)

## Debrecen

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Although you were not here in Hungary personally while you were preaching all over the world, your sincere disciples came here and spread your and Lord Kṛṣṇa’s glory. Although we were not able to hear and see you face to face, we have taken up this responsibility of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness to every conditioned soul by following your disciples’ footsteps. And in this way we are associating with you by following your instructions. You said, “If you really want to please, then distribute my books.” We are trying to follow this instruction in accordance with our abilities, and it is wonderful to see that these books go to the houses of the Hungarian people just like in the other countries.

Only this association with you saves us all from the material world. You are a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, a bona fide spiritual master, the representative of the *guru-paramparā,* our ever well-wisher. Such a well-wisher is our only shelter. This proves your unique greatness: merely your free wish is enough for anyone to reach the perfect platform of life.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us your causeless mercy so that we can always take shelter at your lotus feet, and we will be able to continue and increase our tiny efforts to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Hungary.

*Your eternal servants in Debrecen, Hungary*.

## Denver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We are very grateful to you. You have given us Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Reality. You have given us the formula to be in Kṛṣṇa consciousness constantly. If we try to repay you by serving you in your service to Kṛṣṇa, we accumulate debt because serving you releases the strong hold of *māyā*. Yet we try. We distribute your books. We read your books. We apply the knowledge in your books to our lives. We chant Hare Kṛṣṇa in the streets and in our meditation. We serve your Rādhā-Govinda. We cook and distribute Kṛṣṇa *prasādam*. We hold festivals including full scale Ratha-yātrā. We make Kṛṣṇa conscious friends: our congregation. We raise our kids to love Kṛṣṇa. If we can’t live in your temple we offer our advice, time and mercy to your mission. We want to please you and worship you. In a way you are the same as us and yet you are vastly superior to anyone in the universe because you are from outside the material world – a Kṛṣṇaloka person – and perfectly display pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are a body of devotees that desires to act as your body. We try very hard to please you.

*ISKCON devotees, Denver, Colorado*.

## Detroit

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**”I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Svāmī Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter of His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this Vyāsa-pūjā day honoring your appearance more than anything else I simply want to thank you from the bottom of my heart for bringing the path of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West. If you hadn’t done that, I can honestly say that I do not know where I would be or what I would be doing with my life. But you saved me from a life of superficiality and emptiness – what to speak of a dark and dismal future in who knows how many lifetimes that might have awaited me.

Indeed, you took the order of your spiritual master seriously and made me, and so many others, most fortunate. Practically speaking, everything good that has happened to me has been because of you. My knowledge of my spiritual identity, of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, of Lord Caitanya, and of the previous saints and ācāryas, has been all because of your willingness to take the risk of coming to America. Even on a personal level, the way I now live my life, the way I view this world in the light of spiritual knowledge, the nice devotee friends I now have, the temples I’ve associated with, my travels to India, and so many other things, have all been possible because of you. Furthermore, in my future, all of the preaching I have to do, all of the people I have yet to meet, all of the service unto you and Lord Kṛṣṇa I will do, is all because of your blessings on me. Meeting you and your books was the main event that changed my life. You ruined my material motivation, for which I will always be eternally grateful.

I know, however, that I am a most fallen soul. How you have put up with me the likes of me, I don’t know. And I know that it must look like I am too preoccupied with my self-imposed duties of maintaining myself and family, living the life of a householder. But in my heart, my main concern is that whatever I do, in whatever way I work or earn money, or preach or serve in the temple, my main intent is to somehow widen the circle of influence to spread your mission and this knowledge. Even though my attempt to do this may be filled with faults, it is, nonetheless, my desire and my duty to you. So I am certain that with our continued effort to do this – and, more importantly, with your blessings – we will not fail.

All glories to you, my dearmost spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Detroit

## Encinitas

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace. On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā celebration, we would like to thank you again and again from the cores of our hearts for the incomparable opportunity to be engaged in devotional service to the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu is known as the most merciful incarnation: *namo mahā-vadānyāya kṛṣṇa-prema pradāya te,* because He is freely bestowing love for Kṛṣṇa. As His most intimate devotee, you are perfectly following in His footsteps by freely distributing love for Kṛṣṇa to a degree unparalleled in the history of the world.

In one place you stated that “Lord Caitanya is famous for the deliverance of Jagāi and Mādhāi, and that now in your movement Jagāis and Mādāis are being delivered wholesale.” This ability of Your Divine Grace to rescue countless lost souls from the depths of the ocean of material existence, expertly steer them past innumerable material and so-called spiritual obstacles, carefully delivering them to the eternal shelter of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet, surely indicates your special empowerment by the Lord: *kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā nahe tāra pravartana.*

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the essence of all Vedic knowledge: *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Caitanya-caritāmṛta* and your other transcendental writings. Five thousand years ago, it was detected by Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself that the most essential spiritual knowledge, *Bhagavad-gītā*, had been lost in the course of time. Thus He appeared to again revive it in human society by explaining it to Arjuna. Twenty eight years ago, the Lord’s representative, Your Divine Grace, detected that although there were so many editions of the *Bhagavad-gītā* available, in America and also in India, “none of them can be said to be strictly authoritative because in almost every one of them the commentator has expressed his own opinions without touching the spirit of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is*.”

Thus you gave us your Bhaktivedanta purports, which so perfectly illumine Lord Kṛṣṇa’s instructions. As such, it is now possible, for the first time in history, for each and every person, from any walk of life, to understand this knowledge exactly as Arjuna did, and use it to quickly attain the supreme perfection of life, pure devotional service.

Lord Caitanya, ordered everyone to explain “*kṛṣṇa upadeśa*” – *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, wherever they go and to whomever they meet. Now, by the mercy of Your Divine Grace, these scriptures are available all over the world in nearly every language, thus making it possible for this most important order of Lord Caitanya to be carried out by an ever-increasing number of devotees.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us your wonderful, ever-expanding International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness, “a house in which the whole world can live.” This society of devotees, guided by Your Divine Grace, gives us the facility, protection, association, and enthusiasm we need to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness – in all stages, from preliminary faith to pure love of Kṛṣṇa.

Only by your causeless mercy have we been allowed to join and remain in the association of your devotees. Please forgive our numerous faults and kindly bestow upon us the unflinching desire to take your instructions on our heads as our life and soul, fully dedicating ourselves to your service eternally.

*The servants of your servants in Encinitas, California*.

## Florence

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most dear and beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please let me first offer my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

I throw my useless self in the dust of your lotus feet again and again, hoping one day to become accepted by them as their unalloyed servant. Dancing on the heads of the Kāliya serpent, Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet subdued the snake’s pride and arrogance and forced him, along with his deadly poison, to leave the holy waters of the Yamunā River, restoring the waters to their original quality of crystal-clear purity. Your lotus feet are similarly dancing on top of the multifarious heads of material desire (the desire of wanting to enjoy everything we come upon). This reduces to nil its life air and drives it away from the stream of consciousness flowing from the spirit soul, along with its poisonous, muddying, devastating effects, thus returning to the soul the originally clear awareness of being a pure eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa.

When we say “your lotus feet,” we are comparing their qualities to the qualities of the lotus flower. The most significant difference between them is that although the lotus flower lives in the water, it is not touched by it. On the other hand, your lotus feet are walking all over the dangerous, fierce oceanic waters of the terrible material existence and, miraculously, they have not even become moistened. Whoever takes complete shelter at Your lotus feet can easily cross the tossing waves of the material world and consider himself already situated on the safe holy land of Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma. Any fraction of time spent without serving Your lotus feet is like being in apnea (holding the breath). The inconvenience increases more and more until it becomes intolerable.

Just as the day wraps around the golden sun, this movement shining around your sun-like lotus feet will always keep the path of devotional service clear and safe.

Just as the wheel rotates around the central pivot, Your lotus feet are the central pivot around which this movement will always swiftly and smoothly proceed in its absolutely unique missionary work of bringing the lost spiritual souls to meet again their supreme lover.

Just as the entire existence centers round Śrī Kṛṣṇa, Your lotus feet are the life of our existence both individually and a collectively.

The more the sun moves away from the center of the sky, the more the daylight dims, the shadows become longer, sinking everything into a sort of confused unclear hodge-podge.

If we did not keep your lotus feet high in the sky of your movement, then the ghostly shadows of mental concoctions bring confusion, schisms would tear everything apart and eventually the world would be sunk again into the fearful darkness of nescience.

The more the axle in the wheel becomes decentralized, the more the rotation of the wheel becomes clumsy, unbalanced and obstructive, instead of useful. If we shift your lotus feet from their central position inside ISKCON, our course of action would become awkward, distorted and eventually disturbing to all. The less the consciousness is concentrated on the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the more the nightmare of death enters the existence of the spirit soul.

If we do not concentrate on serving your lotus feet, the incubus for our disappearance would enter the existence of your absolutely indispensable Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I once had a dream in which I was alone on a beautiful white sandy beach with palm trees, calm blue sea and blue sunny sky. But it was as if my body was miniscule in size, and I was observing it all as would a small insect situated on one of the grains of sand. All of a sudden a tumultuous sound could be heard. I saw a cloud of dust approaching from one side of the beach, coming closer and closer. THERE WERE THEIR LORDSHIPS! Lord Caitanya, Lord Nityānanda, more shining than the sun, with all Their associates, with garlands, *karatālas*, *mṛdaṅgas,* conch shells, were dancing in the most ecstatic *kīrtana* ever seen. When the *kīrtana* group reached the place where I was, I saw the beautiful decorated lotus foot of Lord Nityānanda landing on top of me.

I woke up suddenly, overwhelmed by the ecstatic realization that your lotus feet are the manifestation of Lord Nityānanda’s lotus feet. Dancing on top of us, they are transforming our insect-like existence into that of one of Lord Caitanya’s associates, powerfully dragging us into His most transcendental *saṅkīrtana* group.

Even without any qualification from our part, with just a touch of your lotus feet, we can be reinstated in our originally glorious position as the eternal servants of the servants of the servants of the most magnanimous Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I miss you, but I pray that this feeling may increase and increase, more and more, because this same feeling will tie me more and more to your lotus feet. Holding, worshiping, loving, adoring, serving your teachings, your instructions, your Deity, your devotees, your mission, by bringing Your Divine Grace to everyone so that Your Divine Grace can bring everyone back home back to Godhead.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am such a fool that times the world “impossible” tries to find its way into my dictionary, but I am not so foolish to think that something is impossible for you. If you want, you can use us to perform the most incredible tasks.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you will never become an historical figure of the past; a great personality who in the sixties started the Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, died in the late seventies and has now found his place in the memories of a few romantic sentimentalist and whose pictures are printed in the pages of some book. No, you are eternal and everything and everyone factually connected with you is eternally living with you. Your glories will keep expanding unlimitedly in all directions. By the merciful touch of your lotus feet, you will keep rescuing zillions of insect-like fallen souls, reestablishing them at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.

In this movement, we all love you, and we want to remain part of you, continuously holding Your Divine Grace in the center of our every thought, word and action, by preaching what you said and acting as you instructed without adding, subtracting or changing anything from your fully-realized practical conclusions. To say that ISKCON is non-different from you is not just a metaphorical abstraction.

We all pray to the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Kṛṣṇa, from whom remembrance, knowledge and also forgetfulness comes, to please allow us to forever remember and never forget Your Divine Grace’s lotus feet so that wherever we are, in any condition of life, we can always be with Your Divine Grace.

*Your servants at Radio Kṛṣṇa Central, Florence and Terne, Italy.*  
(Written by Tridaṇḍī dāsa)

## Gainesville

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, and all glories to you on your divine appearance day!

We could imagine where we would be now had it not been for your absolute selflessness and immense desire to bring Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the West. We are awed by your greatness and are full of wonder as to how you came here, being so surrendered to Lord Kṛṣṇa. You philosophically convinced thousands of disciples that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead and that by serving His lotus feet we too could feel the bliss and ecstasy you feel every moment for *guru* and Kṛṣṇa. Your patience and determination are unparalleled. Your preaching is music to our ears, the nectar we’ve waited so long to hear. Your instructions are incredibly perfect and fit all circumstances, just as a puzzle fits into its proper place. By example, you have taught us everything we could ever want to know about how to purify ourselves in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and to always strive to become better servants. The least we can do to reciprocate is to intensely preach the glories of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa in every town and village, faithfully follow your orders, purely distribute your books, massively distribute *prasādam*, and constantly strive to become better fixed at your lotus feet. For your pleasure, we distribute over 2,500 plates a week of sumptuous *prasādam* to the students at the University of Florida campus. For almost 20 years we have been distributing *prasādam* just a few feet from where you spoke in 1971.

Thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda, for offering us the opportunity to be servants.

*Your servants,*  
Gainesville Preaching Center  
(Written by Bhakti devī dāsī)

## Gītā-nāgarī

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your divine mission of bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the unhappy conditioned souls of *Kali-yuga*.

As we look around the world today we see that people are very unhappy, being severely afflicted by the threefold miseries. We see fire, floods, earthquakes, severe weather, suicides, mass killings in the name of “ethnic cleansing” and abortion – heinous crimes being committed from the big cities to the most remote villages. People everywhere are in anxiety. When I read a newspaper or hear a news broadcast, I also become fearful... but then I open your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and read: “The bull is the emblem of the moral principle and the cow is the representative of the earth. When the bull and the cow are in a joyful mood, it is to be understood that the people of the world are also in a joyful mood.” These words are so relishable. The solution is so simple. But just yesterday we heard that the scientists are going to extract the cancer-preventing element from the broccoli and inject it into many foods. In this way they will prevent or reduce the risk or cancer. It never ends, the *ugra-karma*. It seems the demons will never stop tampering with the material energy until the entire creation is destroyed. They complicate everything and go to all extents to avoid the simple ways of God... Just protect the cow and the bull, become Kṛṣṇa conscious and be joyful!

Living at your Gītā-nāgarī farm, we feel the heavy pressures of trying to protect cows in this demoniac age in a godless civilization. But we also experience the inner happiness of knowing that we are doing our best to protect the cows and the bulls and to serve Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā Dāmodara.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us everything we need to experience real joy in our lives. Please bless us that we may be successful in pleasing you here at Gītā-nāgarī, and that, somehow or other through our meager endeavors, others may come to know of the importance of the advancement of brahminical culture and cow protection.

*Your servants at Gītā-nāgarī Dhāma, Port Royal, Pennsylvania.*

## Guadalajara

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, today is the most blessed day for all mankind and for all living entities, because you appeared in this material world to give the treasure of devotional service and love of Kṛṣṇa, and in that way you have ended our suffering in this *martya-loka*. Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya has been very merciful with us to send you to save all the sinful people of Kali-yuga. Because you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are *mahā-patita-pāvana*, you have engaged us in the service of the great *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. With that engagement, our lives have changed completely and now we know the real goal of life.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, now all we need is that you do not stop giving us your mercy because without your mercy we cannot maintain our spiritual lives. From the time we took shelter of your lotus feet, we have not known how to serve you and Kṛṣṇa. If you do not instruct us how do it, we will fall back down into the dark well of material life. Please, divine master, do not kick us away from your lotus feet. We know that our service to you has many faults, but we are trying our best.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are grateful to you for your great mercy, kindness, spiritual association, transcendental books, and divine teachings that you have given us out of your causeless mercy. Śrīla Prabhupāda, again, please accept our most humble obeisances millions and millions of times at your divine lotus feet.

*Your most fallen eternal servants of Guadalajara Yātrā. Mexico*.

## Hong Kong

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

In two years it will be your Centennial Celebration year. Your disciples all over the world are preparing for big celebrations. What can we very neophyte devotees here in Hong Kong do for you? It really makes us think.

One difficulty we are facing here is the turning over of devotees. Some of your disciples and grand disciples have left their active services in your ISKCON, although some new devotees have joined over the years. This may be something inevitable as is the case anywhere else. But are we compassionate enough? Is it our fault that our Godbrothers and Godsisters leave? If we are to give a drop of your mercy to the newcomers, then why not to the out goers? Or do they really deserve it?

As we think of reuniting your family, it is a spiritual dilemma in our spiritual life. It makes us more introspective and opens our hearts up to see if we are still harboring some offensive thoughts to you and to your disciples and grand disciples, who may be very conditioned still. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are *patita-pāvana*, savior of the most fallen – please bestow upon us a drop of that mercy too.

To make our hearts receptive to anyone ready to render you service, this, we think, is more important than any plan we can conceive of for your Centennial Celebration. May your fame spread over the three worlds.

*Your disciples and grand disciples in Hong Kong*.

## Imphal

**Śrī Guru Gaurāṅga Jayataḥ**

Most worshipable dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our very humble yet very unqualified obeisances to the dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

The approach of the Centennial year (1996) of your divine appearance seems to be very fast-paced, and so does the fervor of your disciples and followers all over the world to glorify you and make you renowned, in the true sense, as the World ācārya of *sanātana-dharma* in the 20thCentury. So many wonderful events are being organized to fulfill this great ambition of ours. For example, the Second World Congress for the Synthesis of Science and Religion, being hosted by the Bhaktivedanta Institute in January of 1996 in Calcutta, the place of your divine appearance, under the visionary and able guidance of one of your most dear and confidential disciples, H.H. Bhaktisvarūpa Dāmodara Svāmī. It is aimed at establishing the scientific basis of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the thing you most desire to happen. The absolute dedication of those for whom your full satisfaction is the only goal in life, is most encouraging for us in our very humble service to fulfill your cause.

Although we have been lagging way behind in our endeavors to properly glorify Your Divine Grace and fulfill your desires to the optimum, this time we all have resolved with adamant determination to undertake and accomplish the following tasks – to participate in the celebration of this *Centennial of the Century*, to justify to some extent the worth of our being your humble followers.

1\. Organizing a Grand *saṅkīrtana padayātrā* to spread the real peace formula of the supreme authority, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, as propagated by Your Divine Grace, covering the seven northeastern states of India, starting from Imphal, Manipur and reaching Calcutta, the place of your divine appearance by March of 1996.

2\. Publishing your complete biography in parts in the prominent newspapers of Manipur during the whole year of 1996 to help the people have a thorough glimpse of your matchless sacrifice, and, contribution of spiritual yet practical guidance to relieve humanity from the ongoing turmoil.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we hear that you desire a very big ISKCON Manipuri temple established in Imphal to encourage and enliven the pure ideal *bhāgavata* culture in Manipur, which could spread from there to the rest of the world. For your pleasure, we are glad and very inspired to declare that our vigor for the Centennial Celebration will not subside until this desire of Your Divine Grace is fully materialized.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg for your special blessings so that we can do our utmost to accomplish the above tasks just for your satisfaction.

*Begging to remain in the service of your servants eternally*,  
ISKCON Imphal, Manipur, India.

## Ireland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Today we got together and talked about your glories and wrote some of our feelings of gratitude for your kindly coming to save us from a life of suffering in material existence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are inspiring us all; you influence our lives in every respect. By your divine mercy we have everything: the association of devotees and engagement in devotional service.

The temple here is just one of many buildings in a big city, but by your mercy thousands of people here are being effected. By just a little contact with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our own lives have become all-auspicious and you are now engaging us in passing on that mercy by distributing your books and preaching in other ways.

Thinking of you, serving you, and remembering your words, we feel you are with us, wishing us well. Hearing your voice on tapes we feel safe, reading your books it’s like we have known you before, like you are telling us something that we know but have lost and forgotten. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always there to help us, and you know what is best for us now and in the future.

We are mostly young devotees here, we want to learn from you, to be enthusiastic and loyal. It is so kind of you to allow us to be busy in your mission to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You are so soft-hearted and merciful, so patient with us, your children. Śrīla Prabhupāda we beg that we can carry on serving according to your desire.

*The unworthy servants of your servants,*  
Hare Kṛṣṇa Cultural Center, Republic of Ireland

## Kiev

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances, thrown in the dust of your lotus feet! All glories to you, greatest personality, savior or this fallen world.

Like Lord Varāha, you have lifted this planet from the dark depth of the ocean of nescience and elevated it to the shining sun of real knowledge. O most kind one, you have bestowed to the people this sun, the Vedic scriptures, with your own magnificent commentary done in accordance with the Vaiṣṇava tradition. As a result, darkness is retreating, being dispelled, and everything is transformed, being lit-up with its transcendental effulgence. Blind men begin to see, helpless find strength and faith, and the divine symphony of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, the supreme master, is sounding over the planet more and more majestically.

Your worldwide divine mission is astonishing. Who else could foresee that this place known as Earth, which was dark due to Western people’s sinfulness, could be turned into a blossoming garden where happy mankind sings and dances in the bliss of pure divine consciousness? Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu said that a time will come when Kṛṣṇa’s name will be chanted in every town and village. This statement seemed to your Godbrothers just allegory. But your faith in Lord Caitanya’s prediction and the instructions of your spiritual master was always unwavering. Who is able to appreciate this faith? Who is aware of the depth of your devotion to the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Prabhupāda – so much that you were able to spread the pure transcendental message of Kṛṣṇa? It is only by your causeless mercy that all fallen souls can obtain a drop of divine mercy from the flow of pure love of Godhead emanating from the lotus heart of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

So let us be engaged in this most important and necessary mission in all the three worlds to distribute love of God through the production and distribution of your wonderful books. Please bless us to serve you throughout our lives in this way, surrendering to this mission more and more. Let this service be constant and uninterrupted. Your blessings are everything for us. Please grant us the mercy to be engaged in your service eternally through the service of your brilliant disciple. Thank you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your eternal servants from Kiev, Ukraine.*

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Devotional service begins with faith. That faith is given by you, and you are the object of faith. The Absolute Truth is only revealed to persons who have such faith. Our faith in others is based on their love for you and their service and obedience to you. Our only solace in life are the devotees who have faith in you and have dedicated their life and soul to your service. Only sincere service which is pleasing to you has any substance. All other activities are pseudo-devotion, at best, and can only cause a disturbance.

I have nothing to show for my many years of so-called service, neither devotion nor accomplishments. I can only pray that you will keep me on and allow me to actually serve you one day.

On your divine appearance day, we are praying for the intelligence to make our lives pure and simple, with all of our activities and thoughts dedicated to the service of yourself and Śrī Pañca-tattva. We are praying for the perfection of life – attachment to your lotus feet – which fulfills all desires.

We are praying for the strength to preach and for the clarity of mind to understand how to preach properly according to time, place and circumstance. Please give us just a drop of your vision, compassion and enthusiasm. Let your mission be alive in us.

*Your aspiring servants of ISKCON, Laguna Beach*.

## London (Soho)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this blessed day, the ninety-eight anniversary of your auspicious appearance in this world.

On December 14th1969, you invited Lord Kṛṣṇa to take up residence in London along with His consort, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. You called Him Londonīśvara – the controller of London. We all know that you are particularly fond of Londonīśvara – “that nice boy.” You had some extraordinary pastimes with Him. You kidnapped Him and you regularly visited Him – even as you were preparing to pass from this world.

Now, twenty-five years later, due to our incredible good fortune, we have been entrusted with the responsibility of serving Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. Some marketing company hoping to capitalize on our 25thAnniversary sent some advertisement to us regarding souvenir pens. They congratulated us on having lasted 25 years. They said that 95% of companies which started in 1969 no longer exist. The credit for our survival must go to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and Kṛṣṇa. It is certainly not due to our managing ability. Somehow, you have installed a deep desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the hearts of all your followers. Your depth of character still convinces many souls to dedicate their lives in this way, despite you physical absence.

We wish we could say that we had achieved more on your behalf, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in the way that you served your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura Prabhupāda. We are still very much children in the matter of spiritual understanding. But we are learning. And we are confident that with your continued favor on us we will one day build a temple for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara of which you would be proud.

Over the years we’ve offered more than 55,000 āratis to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara. They enjoy a very high standard of worship – an abundance of fresh flowers, gorgeous outfits made by Līlā-śakti Prabhu and her helpers, and the most delicious *bhoga* offerings in the world.

We’ve distributed more than 4,000,000 books in London and the South of England. Many, many people visit our temple here and take *darśana* of Their Lordships – their lives forever changed. Many have chosen not to leave but to become your soldiers in the fight against māyā.

Through our restaurant, lunch program and Food for Life program more than 5,500,000 plates of Kṛṣṇa *prasādam* have been distributed.

There have been more than 8,000 *hari-nāmas* and 26 Ratha-yātrā Festivals for the glory of Lord Jagannātha, whom you also introduced to England. The holy names “Hare Kṛṣṇa” are household words in England. Devotees are accepted as an integral part of London.

These are but a few of the measurable achievements – there are countless other effects of the spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the fruits of which we may only begin to appreciate in due course of time.

But who is due to the credit for all of this? You are, Śrīla Prabhupāda. If it wasn’t for your kindness, none of us would be here to assist you in this mission. Our only credit is that we’ve learned from you to share the mercy that you have bestowed upon us.

You’ve caused all of this to happen. How? Because all of this has been done for your pleasure, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Is there anyone in the history of the world who has motivated so many people – from a myriad of varied backgrounds – to dedicate themselves to such a noble cause?

And what was your secret? How have you managed to motivate so many people in this way? By your wonderful qualities – your kindness, thoughtfulness, compassion, broadness of heart and magnanimity, strictness and intolerance of ignorance, humor and humanity, virtue and integrity, strength and softness, humility and respectfulness, wisdom, patience and understanding – Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are incomparable.

We’re planning a birthday party in November at which we are trying to gather all of your followers who’ve participated in the London temple throughout the years. We don’t intend to merely reminisce, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Rather, we want to take a leaf out of your book and always look forward to newer and greater conquests – never resting on your laurels.

Gradually we are learning that it is your purity of motive that makes you different from others, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We aspire to be like you – not by means of imitation, but by learning how to humbly tread the path you trod. You spent a lifetime in preparation, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We should not expect to become successful overnight by some quick-fix technique. We must strive for that primary greatness of character that you have, then we will really be able to please you. And that will be our real success.

Please bless us to continue serving you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please make us worthy to serve Your Lordships, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara, let we spoil everything by our childish notions.

*Your servants in London*.

## London (South)

Pūjya parama-guru Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept the humble obeisances of your grand disciples at the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who literally saved millions of souls from drowning in this miserable ocean of birth and death.

You could so easily have retired from all activities and simply immersed yourself in Kṛṣṇa-kathā in Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma, leaving us foolish conditioned souls in the darkness of ignorance. Instead you ventured to the West and, in the process, transformed the whole world into Vṛndāvana. People all over the whole world are attracted by the limitless qualities that you possess, some of which are listed below:

Savior of the whole world,  
Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s favorite son,  
Inspiring so many to take up Kṛṣṇa consciousness,  
Life of the lifeless,  
Abandoning Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma for hell,  
Protector of ISKCON,  
Radiating an aura that attracts everyone,  
Always teaching us the highest philosophy,  
Blessing the whole world,  
Hope for the hopeless,  
Utilizing everything in Kṛṣṇa’s service,  
Provider of a house in which the whole world can live,  
Accepting us even with all our faults and defects,  
Destroyer of the egos of the egoistic,  
Acknowledging whatever little service we do for Kṛṣṇa,  
Knower of the absolute truth – Śrī Kṛṣṇa,  
Ignoring the offenses committed at your lotus feet,  
Joyfully transforming the whole world into Vṛndāvana,  
Always destroying the ignorance of the ignorant,  
Immersing yourself in spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

There were certainly many Vaiṣṇavas in India who were devoted, learned, and renounced. However, the fact remains that you alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, were sufficiently qualified to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Only you had sufficient faith in the instructions of Lord Caitanya, the order of your spiritual master and the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa, to seriously attempt spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness outside of India. Only you had compassion and vision to preach the message of Lord Caitanya to those who most needed it. Only one of the topmost confidential devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa could perform such an extraordinary task. By your unparalleled achievements it is clear that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, occupy a unique position in the history of Vaiṣṇavism.

You were empowered to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a practical and straightforward way appropriate for the modern world. Without changing or compromising the teachings of the Lord even slightly, you presented the confidential truths in clear and intelligible manner, suitable for both the layman and the scholar.

Your success, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is itself proof that your endeavors are approved, ordained and blessed by the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself.

You are our eternal spiritual grandfather. Although we commit countless mistakes and offenses just as children do, please forgive us. We beg that you will continue to give us your mercy.

*Your worthless grand disciples at the South London Center, England*.  
(Written by Nabhinandana dāsa and Jayanti devī dāsī)

## Los Angeles

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*pṛthivite āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe more nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**”In as many towns and villages as there are on the surface of the earth, My Holy Name will be preached.” (*****Caitanya-caritāmṛta, ādi-Lilā*** **17.203)**

Faith in the words of the Supreme Lord is certainly the life of the devotees. It is such faith that motivates the devotee to rise and serve the Lord each day. But the śāstric prediction that the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa would be spread all over the world had long stymied staunch devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. “Of course, the prophecy would be fulfilled,” they reasoned. “The holy name would indeed be spread all over the world, but how? Who had the faith to act on these words?”

It took a very special pure devotee, one with extraordinary strong faith, to act on these words of the Lord. These words cried out for His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda looked to the words of the *śāstra* with faith, and Lord Caitanya showed him how.

Śrīla Prabhupāda did not see the words of *śāstra* as flowery words simply meant to dramatize Lord Caitanya’s advent. He understood them to be the infallible desire of the Lord. Thus, to Śrīla Prabhupāda these words were never really a prediction at all. They only appear like a foretelling to us because we perceive a gap of time between when they were spoken and when they are fulfilled. Śrīla Prabhupāda never perceived such a gap, instead he had faith.

Śrīla Prabhupāda looked to the order of his *guru* with faith, and Lord Caitanya gave him all facility.

At the time of his first meeting with Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, Śrīla Prabhupāda received the instruction to preach in the English language. Śrīla Prabhupāda understood that within this order of his spiritual master lay all facility needed for preaching the holy name all over the world. Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s order had made the course of his life clear, and therefore Śrīla Prabhupāda spent his entire life preaching in English with faith that all other arrangements would be made.

Śrīla Prabhupāda looked to the task ahead with faith, and Lord Caitanya used him as the perfect instrument.

As Śrīla Prabhupāda descended from the Jaladuta to begging Lord Caitanya’s worldwide movement, he didn’t know whether to turn left or right, but he had faith. At Boston Harbor we wrote: “My dear Lord Kṛṣṇa, You are so kind upon this useless soul, but I do not know why you have brought me here. Now You can do whatever You like with me. But I guess You have some business here, otherwise, why would You bring me to this terrible place? Somehow or other, O Lord, You have brought me here to speak about You. Now, my Lord, it is up to You to make me a success or failure, as You like... O Lord, I am just like a puppet in Your hands. So if You have brought me here to dance, then make me dance, make me dance, O Lord, make me dance as You like. I have no devotion, nor do I have any knowledge, but I have strong faith in the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda looked to the future with faith, and Lord Caitanya revealed to him the complete fulfillment of the prophecy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda began alone and penniless, he had only faith. Yet, within ten years he had laid the foundation for the Golden Age of Lord Caitanya all over this planet. As if that wasn’t enough, Śrīla Prabhupāda’s faith urged him on. In 1976 Śrīla Prabhupāda wrote to his GBC disciples, “Over the past ten years I have given the framework and now we have become more than the British Empire. Even the British Empire was not as expansive as we are. They had only a portion of the world, and we have not completed expanding. We must expand more and more unlimitedly.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we look to you with faith that we may assist you as you continue to fulfill the prophecy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

*Your servants at New Dvārakā, Los Angeles, California*.

## Malmo

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept our humble obeisances at the divine dust of your lotus like feet. We are trying to serve your lotus feet and your mission; therefore, we humbly request you to please accept this humble presentation of our Vyāsa-pūjā offering.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
because you did not have any other desire or ambition  
than to fulfill your *guru’s* orders and mission,  
you received his benediction giving you divine vision  
to develop the worldwide preaching mission  
in fulfillment of Śrīman Mahāprabhu’s desire and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda’s prediction.

Your divine mission is like the sunrise – benediction  
from the darkness of many lives blinded by Kali-yuga’s sinful mission.  
The touch of the rays of the sun of your divine empowered vision  
causes the unfolding, like a lotus flower, of your worldwide divine mission.

Because you are completely surrendered to your *guru’s* mission,  
you were the only one to inherit his divine title and vision,  
showing that you are the *ācārya* in Śrīman Mahāprabhu’s saṅkīrtana mission,  
brooking the old and staged sectarian visions.

You are the only one who can give worldwide preaching vision,  
saving millions of conditioned lives by your worldwide mission.  
Others may remain in one corner in the world talking about the inner vision,  
while the world is going to hell due to Kali-yuga’s horrible missions.

Instead, from one corner, you spread your preaching to a worldwide mission,  
giving millions hope for an eternal life and knowledge by blissful  
saṅkīrtana of Śrī Caitanya’s moon of benediction.

You have given everything necessary to complete this human form’s mission,  
and you have left behind your divine writing vision  
to spread Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission.

You have said:

”If one is fortunate enough to transcend all this so-called goodness and badness and come to the platform of devotional service by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa and the *guru*, his life becomes successful. In this regard, one must be very bold so that he can conquer these enemies of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Not caring for good or bad of this material world, one must boldly propagate Kṛṣṇa consciousness.” (*Bhāg*. 7.15.43-44)

”... the sword of knowledge is given by Kṛṣṇa, and when one serves the guru and Kṛṣṇa in order to hold the sword of Kṛṣṇa’s instructions, Balarāma gives one strength. Balarāma is Nityānanda*: brajendra-nandana yei, śaci-suta hoilo sei, balarāma hoilo nitāi.* This *bala* – Balarāma – comes with Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and both of Them are so merciful in this Age of Kali. One may easily take shelter of Their lotus feet. They come specifically to deliver the fallen souls of this age. *Pāpī tāpī yata chila harināme uddhārila*. Their weapon is *saṅkīrtana, hari-nāma*. We are therefore worshiping Kṛṣṇa Balarāma in Vṛndāvana. In the *Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad* (3.2.4), it is stated:

*nāyam ātmā bala-hīnena lābhyo*  
*na ca pramādāt tapaso vāpy aliṅgāt*  
*etair upāyair yatate yas tu vidvāṁs*  
*tasyaisa ātmā viśate brahma-dhāma*

**"One cannot attain the goal of life without the mercy of Balarāma".**
