Śrīla Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura therefore says, *nitāiyer koruṇā habe, braje rādhā-kṛṣṇa pābe:* when one receives the mercy of Balarāma, Nityānanda, one can attain the lotus feet of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa very easily.

*se sambandha nāhi yāra, bṛthā janma gelo tāra*  
*vidyā-kule ki koribe tāra*

**”If one has no connection with Nitāi, Balarāma, then even though one is a very learned scholar, or jñānī or he has taken birth in a very respectable family, these assets will not help him. We must therefore conquer the enemies of Kṛṣṇa consciousness with the strength received from Balarāma.” (*****Bhāg*****. 7.15.45)**

**”Therefore if we simply place ourselves at the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa by taking to Kṛṣṇa consciousness and keeping always in touch with Him by chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra, we need not take trouble in arranging to return to the spiritual world by the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, this is very easy.” (*****Bhāg*****. 7.15.53)**

*From your aspiring servants in Malmo, Sweden.*

## Manchester

Another year is suddenly passed.  
What sign of certain service done?  
Not with the mind the battle won  
to prove proclaimed love at last.

Another year the yearning heart  
could conjure convoluted things,  
soar aloft on hope’s wide wings,  
yet from true love in pain depart.

But truth reveals darkness there,  
Dark shadowed dreams all born of fear.  
Deceiving visions seeming dear,  
concocting castles in the air.

Therein to dwell and while the time,  
for all illusion’s work to waste.  
Once more within a body placed,  
still guilty of that ancient crime.

But here a day from year to year,  
returns us to reality,  
and urged towards eternity,  
we break the bonds of mortal sphere.

A day when we remember well,  
how near a hundred years ago,  
a lost love came again to show,  
remission, when we would rebel.

A hundred years all passed apace.  
And have you gone? How shall we tell?  
For hardly had we known you well,  
to know you now within your grace.

But still you are for all to feel,  
seen in every sincere thought,  
found whenever frankly sought,  
forever unto us revealed.

And yet from time to time we see  
corporeal identity,  
brief glimpses of divinity.  
All born of causeless charity.

What hope was there for us to find  
that loving Lord who led you here?  
What fortune that we could be near  
a soul so rare, a love sublime?

So more than ever this day we,  
pause to reevaluate,  
align again where aberrant,  
our aim to perfect purity.

Let legion upon legion lay  
before your feet. A hope held dear.  
Seems nearer as we see each year,  
the growing grandeur of this day.

What fame and glory is not theirs  
who on this day remember yours,  
and who by every effort cause  
all the worlds to be aware?

But helplessly we can but weep,  
for hardly can we save one soul,  
this one who now before you falls  
to pray your grace this place to keep.

Dear Prabhupāda, our plea remains.  
Accept the helpless supplicant,  
whose faith and love are far distant,  
and still has bare a rightful claim.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. All glories to your wonderful mission. Thank you for allowing us another opportunity to offer this our paltry and wholly insufficient praise. Perhaps by so doing we will become purified. Then we can gradually come to really understand some of your glory, and thus offer meaningful prayers.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our real debt is to you alone. Although there may be many other advanced Vaiṣṇavas, it was you alone who rose up and sailed forth to save us. But for that we would now be nicely rotting in hell. At this point in time that fact is so clear that we feel quite unable to credit anyone else with our salvation. Whether right or wrong, that is how we feel. Perhaps with time we may realize some deeper truth about you; how you are revealing yourself through your servant. Indeed we pray for this profound realization. Whatever it may take to guide us to your feet, please bestow that. We only have our breath to offer. Please take that in whatever way you see fit. We only want to serve your mission. Please keep us within ISKCON. We know we can never be qualified to directly approach you, but by remaining within your society we feel your divine presence and are satisfied. We thank you from the bottom of our hearts for your unlimited kindness and we hope that one day we shell do something to show some evidence of our gratitude.

*Your helpless servants in Manchester, Engla*nd.

## Manipur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear most Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to your Divine Grace.

On this most auspicious day, we would like to thank you for giving us the opportunity to perform devotional service at your divine lotus feet. It is very merciful of you to have arranged things so that we have nothing to do but serve your servants. You are a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra* who descended on this earth to fulfill the mission of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Being a perfect disciple, you have fulfilled the instruction of your spiritual master.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave us the real life, which is the science of self-realization and Kṛṣṇa-*prem*ā. Actually, we are insignificant grandsons and granddaughters. Whatever service we may perform is also very little, but we try to please you by our preaching efforts. Although we are unqualified neophyte devotees, we are sure that by Your Divine Grace, we will come to know you. This we will accomplish by serving your disciples who are nicely carrying out your order to preach throughout the whole world by publishing and distributing millions of your incomparable books.

In your purports to the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (9.22.32) you have stated: “The Kṛṣṇa consciousness can be well spread in the state of Manipur by the cooperative efforts of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness devotees. If the Vaiṣṇava spirit is revived there, it will be a wonderful place, renowned throughout the entire world.” By your divine blessing, you have sent capable Vaiṣṇavas who can carry out your divine mission very nicely. Being descendants of saintly persons, all the Vaiṣṇavas of Manipur will continue their Vaiṣṇava spirit, revitalized by your unequivocal and detailed instructions. By your mercy, we, the fallen neophyte devotees of ISKCON Manipur, shall be able to have direct association with Their Lordships Śrī Śṛī Rādhā Kṛṣṇacandra.

*Your most unworthy servants at the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā Kṛṣṇacandra, Maṇi-Mandira, Manipur, India.*  
(by Ekanātha dāsa, Temple President)

## Markham

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You are so great and so pure,  
Lord Kṛṣṇa sent you to give us the cure.

Lord Kṛṣṇa sent you to complete Lord Caitanya’s mission;  
only you had the determination and the vision.

From birth there were signs that you would be great,  
and even the astrologers revealed your fate.

You knew from the beginning what had to be done,  
and took on the task with the heart of a lion.

You diligently wrote, printed, and then distributed, even in the cold,  
so the greatest philosophy could be told.

You took Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī’s instruction,  
and followed it with complete and utter devotion.

To spread Śrī Caitanya’s message,  
you made the long ocean passage.

You left your beloved Vṛndāvana,  
just to give us all salvation.

With only Kṛṣṇa at your side,  
you came to New York’s East Side.

Your message was so sweet,  
thousands soon fell at your lotus feet.

Nothing could compare  
to the purports you would share.

You showed everyone what is was to have a feast,  
knowing that we would take *prasādam* at the very least.

You showed us that you were the bonafide teacher,  
and that the rest were all cheaters.

You taught us that books are the basis,  
and had everyone distributing by the cases.

You spread *hari-nāma* from coast to coast,  
for it was Kṛṣṇa’s holy name that should be foremost.

You became our guiding light,  
in this material world that is as dark as the night.

You taught us how to worship the Deities,  
showing that devotion, love, and respect are the necessities.

In twelve years you showed us more,  
than anyone had ever done before.

Around the world Kṛṣṇa’s name spread,  
all due to ISKCON that you so boldly led.

Temples opened everywhere with the touch of your hand,  
nothing could stop the Hare Kṛṣṇa’s spreading across the land.

By your merciful glance,  
we have been given the greatest chance.

Serving you and Śrī Śrī Śyāmasundara,  
we know nothing could be better.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we really pray,  
that whatever may happen day to day;

Whatever trials and tests that we may meet,  
allow us to remain serving at your lotus feet.

It is clear for us to see,  
that it is you we wish to serve through eternity.

We bow at your lotus feet, time and time again,  
and it is there that we wish to forever remain.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is all we can say,  
all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on each and every day,  
all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in each and every way.

All we can say for you who had us saved,  
is “Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! Jaya Gurudeva!”

*Your humble servants in Markham, Canada*  
(Written by Mīnākṣī devī dāsī)

## Melbourne

**Śrīla Prabhupāda Preaches to Australia**

Australia slept in Kali’s cold night,  
The seventies had just begun;  
By your kindness you came to wake them  
With the rays of the Kṛṣṇa sun.

”Why have you come,” asked the barking dogs,  
”To this prosperous and lucky nation?”  
But you saw no difference, that ‘this is Australia.’  
Only fools make demarcation.

”As a salesman travels widely,  
Rich customers to find,  
Let the first class men of your land  
Intelligently apply their mind.”

Preaching to skeptics at Wayside Chapel:  
”Take to this process” you implored.  
”What good is this chanting?” one angry man challenged.  
”It saves you from death!” you roared.

One night a boy clamed to be God,  
And you challenged, and swooped like a hawk.  
”Can you become President Nixon?” “Yes I can!”  
”Then become! First become, and then talk.”

With logic and argument you continued the fight:  
”If I kick your face can you protect?”  
”Yes I can!” “”By whose power?” By my power as a human!”  
You immediately revealed the defect.

”If you have human power don’t claim to be God,  
please don’t be misled in this way.”  
With compassion and love you beseeched the young boy  
”Become Godly, this is the way.”

Palais Theatre hosts servitor Godhead  
Wielding preacher’s mace.  
Discourse done, man asks:  
”Are you enjoying you stay, Your Grace?”

”Yes, I enjoy everywhere,  
And I’ll explain to you why:  
I’m Kṛṣṇa’s servant, every land is His  
This fact you can’t deny.”

In theatres and campuses, colleges, quiet beaches,  
Town Halls and packed Temples you spoke  
The very same message passed down through millennia,  
Spiritual rebirth to invoke:

”We are touring the world with the very same message,  
We have no other business, please listen.  
”Please try to love God, and then you’ll be happy.  
In a nutshell this is our mission.

”If you love God, you love everyone,  
That is love perfect.  
No love of God means suffering.  
That’s the world’s defect.

”Twenty years of flapping flags,  
United Nations’ story.  
One God, one prayer, under Kṛṣṇa’s banner,  
This is real spiritual glory!”

Melbourne Airport, joyous greeting,  
Devotees’ warm rejoice.  
”But why heads pressed to the floor?  
And why the new Rolls Royce?”

Though *Age* reporters craved hot scandal  
You gave them cool-headed facts:  
”I simply collect obeisances  
Like the tax man collects tax.”

”Kṛṣṇa rides in a golden car.  
This rubber and tin’s a bluff!  
*Guru* represents the Supreme Lord –  
Rolls Royce is not enough!”

”Your Grace, you claim to be ascetics.  
Self-denial’s the way to live!”  
”We can spend all the money of the world.  
Unfortunately, you do not give!”

Your powerful preaching has no end,  
We have glimpsed a tiny part.  
Let the research continue!  
Please allow me to cleanse my blackened heart.

I pray that you limitless pastimes  
Be revealed through careful churning,  
And pour like cooling nectar  
Into Australian hearts a-burning.

Though diseased and fallen taste nectar is bitter,  
O master, let them taste it as sweet!  
Today I pray: “Please give this sun burnt country  
The shade of your lotus feet.”

*Your servants in Melbourne, Australia*  
(Written by Kūrma dāsa)

## Mexico City

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which are the shelter that saves us from birth and death and bestows upon us the opportunity for pure devotional service. It is our sincere desire on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā to intone a prayer of gratitude. We wish to show you how thankful we are for all the wonderful things which you have given us for being happy and successful in this life, while simultaneously attaining the ultimate goal of life – *prema-bhakti* – pure love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

You gave us *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana* – the congregational chanting of the holy names of the Lord – along with your profound and perfect instructions in the shape of your sacred books. You also engaged us in the massive distribution of your books, which helps one to achieve the greatest benefits that can only be obtained by self-realized souls, and which delivers suffering humanity. The Vedic scriptures inform us that no one can make progress in spiritual life in a tangible manner unless the spiritual master is pleased and bestows his blessings. You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have told us clearly how we can please you: staying united and working cooperatively within ISKCON, distributing your books in unlimited quantities and becoming pure devotees of Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this glorious and auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā we prostrate ourselves before your divine lotus feet and humbly pray that you give us your blessings to be able to eternally remain your servants and that you give us the necessary ability and intelligence to do things in such a way that you may be satisfied.

*Your servants in the temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-gopāla, Mexico City, Mexico*.

## Miami

Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious occasion of your appearance in this world, we naturally reflect within our minds on the wonderful gifts you have left for us. You have given us this wonderful ISKCON movement to help us go back to Godhead. This Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is cent per cent spiritual, an extension of the eternal pastimes of Lord Caitanya. You knew that unless we could experience a spiritual taste higher and more exciting than our previous mundane experiences, we would have no chance to remain in this movement to become pure devotees. As we are struggling daily to purify ourselves, we can experience the wonderful adventure of becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

This movement has all the makings of a great adventure, just as in Kṛṣṇa-*līlā* and Caitany-*līlā*. In trying to spread this movement we must face powerful demons who would like to destroy us. We sometimes face opponents within our movement and even within our minds. Again and again we see that when we are almost defeated, Lord Kṛṣṇa steps in and reverses the situation, just as He defeated many demons in Vṛndāvana. He purifies our own consciousness. He rectifies the faults and shortcomings within this movement. He transforms our greatest apparent defeats into our greatest victories. In this way, He intensifies our meditation on Him and our love for you. This is exactly the mood of the eternal *lī\\lās* of Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya.

In the spiritual world, whatever one desires becomes immediately fulfilled. Since this is a spiritual movement, if our desires are pure, then whatever we desire will become manifest. This movement is not bound by any mundane laws. If the Lord desires, He can give us billions of dollars immediately. What is the difficulty for Him? If He desires, millions of devotees will join this movement. Yet we sometimes find ourselves bewildered by the problem of how to pay the temple’s bills or how to pay for multimillion dollar lawsuits. We sometimes reduce our service to the Deities or eliminate some temple programs because too many devotees have left the movement. But Lord Kṛṣṇa is waiting to give us the facility to serve Him as soon as He sees that our desires have become pure.

You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have told us that as long as we continue to chant our rounds and follow the regulative principles, we will not have any financial difficulties. Why then do we have so many difficulties within this movement? If we look at ourselves, we will see how many of us are not keeping our vows. Lord Kṛṣṇa is eagerly waiting to give us the whole world – as soon as we follow your instructions.

Lord Kṛṣṇa secretly hid His Kaustubha gem with the treasury of Kāliya so that when the defeated Kāliya serpent was ready to worship the Lord with valuable paraphernalia, he would offer the most valuable gem to the Lord, thinking it to be his own. Similarly, earth’s unlimited hidden wealth all belongs to Kṛṣṇa, and by continuing to purify ourselves and the world by this *saṅkīrtana* movement, that wealth will eventually fall into the hands of sincere devotees, who will offer it back to the Lord by engaging it properly in His service. It remains there only for that purpose.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have given us a wonderful gift in the form of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. If we simply carry out your instructions and work cooperatively to spread this movement, we will relish excitement and bliss as we watch this movement expand by the Lord’s mercy. You have given us a golden opportunity to live very happy, exciting lives as we purify ourselves in preparation for going back home, back to Godhead. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your humble servants in Miami, Florida.*

## Montreal

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to your divine lotus feet which are the only shelter for all the suffering conditioned souls. You are like the rising moon which soothes the burning heart with its cooling rays. Your instructions, your pictures, your voice, your disciples and all your sincere followers all serve to bring us closer to your divine lotus feet which alone can give us all protection.

Just yesterday some unknown voice called on the telephone: “Mahārāja, I am finally calling you after having had your telephone number for the last two weeks. I am a fallen devotee. I am not yet initiated, but I have known ISKCON and Śrīla Prabhupāda for the last 15 years. In the last 10 years I have traveled outside of Canada and have done and seen many things. Now I want to come back and make things right. I need your help. I need a friend. Materially I have what I need, but I am not happy. I know that Lord Kṛṣṇa and Śrīla Prabhupāda speak the Absolute Truth. Thank you for listening to me. Can I come to see you? I really want to make things right and wish to help ISKCON.”

Such souls abound in the material world. All who have come in contact with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can never forget you, can never forget Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Sooner or later they have to come back.

This last week I was fortunate to give a series of lectures at *le College Francais* here in Montreal City, not far from where you lived when you stayed in Montreal near McGill University in 1968. For four consecutive hours, we were allowed to teach three and four classes a day to groups of 30-35 students – in total, some 400 students. All learned to chant the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahāmantra,* all got to hear the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, received literature on the philosophy and all got to take Kṛṣṇa-prasādam in the form of cookies. On the last day of classes, one of the professors at this Christian school who was teaching there for the last 30 years asked the following question: “Sir, in your denomination, do you pray for one another?” When I replied “yes, prayer is part of our devotional activities,” he replied: “Then can I humbly request you to pray for me? Please think of me in your prayers. I do counseling at this college, and I always meet so many students who are undergoing so much suffering. Please pray for me so I can help them.” This same professor would take the cookie *prasādam* every day and liked them very much. When he saw that I had quite a few left (about 40), he asked if he could take them all so that he could distribute them to the students who come to see him.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here at the Montreal Temple, Lord Kṛṣṇa is arranging for many students to come in contact with us. The Province of Quebec, the only French province in the whole Canada and North America, holds *the world record for the highest rate of suicides on a per capita basis among young people.* These are frightening statistics.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, only Kṛṣṇa consciousness can save humanity. Please allow us to become more instrumental in changing the present degraded mentality among the people. Please allow for more and more people to come in contact with your divine instructions so that their hearts can melt, and their prolonged suffering can finally come to an end.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us all this unique opportunity to engage in your devotional service. Please help us preach your glories more and more.

*Your servants, ISKCON Montreal, Canada*.  
(Written by Bhakti Rāghava Swami)

## Murāri-sevaka

**Śrīla Prabhupāda Sevāṣṭakam**

*yo megha-varṇa-puruṣaṁ puruṣārtha-dātā*  
*tvad-dṛṣṭi-spṛṣṭa-patitādhika-vipra-varyaḥ*  
*svāhlāda-tyakta-guru-sevana-tāpa-labdhaḥ*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ‘smi*

**”He endowed humanity with the priceless goal of life, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, whose bodily hue resembles that of a lustrous monsoon cloud. The touch of his merciful glance elevates the most fallen of men to a platform superior to the most excellent** ***brāhmaṇa*****. He abandoned his personal happiness of residing in Śrī Vṛndāvana Dhāma and accepted countless troubles in order to render service to his guru, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*dharmasya roga-śamanārtha-bhava-prasādāt*  
*pāścātya-deśa-taraṇāgata-vāri-dūtaḥ*  
*ācāra-hīna-śaraṇaḥ vyasanārdra-rāgaḥ*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ’smi*

**”He mercifully came with the purpose of curing** ***dharma*****, who had become diseased. Thus he boarded the steamship Jaladūta to deliver the Western countries. He became the shelter for those totally bereft of decent behavior, his heart melted out of love for the souls in such a distressed condition. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*śrī-nāma-jāyu-pariṣevana-dhairya-kāryaḥ*  
*strī-sūna-pāna-dhana-vañcana-jīvadāryaḥ*  
*gaurāṅga-pāda-kamalārpaṇa-yoga-vaktā*  
*sevām pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṇkaro ‘smi*

**”That noble person accepted the duty of a physician and patiently administered the medicine of the holy names of Kṛṣṇa to those addicted to promiscuity, meat-eating, intoxication and gambling. He preached the** ***yoga*** **of offering everything to the lotus feet of Lord Gaurāṅga. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*tattvāndha-loka-viṣayendriya-saṁvṛtānāṁ*  
*gītopadeśa-paṭhanāhnika-dīpa-dātā*  
*dīkṣānuśikṣa-japa-mantra-vidhāna-dhātā*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ’smi*

**”For those blind to the truth, absorbed in the world of the senses and sense objects, he illuminated their consciousness through the daily reading of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s instructions in the** ***Bhagavad-gītā*****. He gave them initiation and instruction in the process of chanting the** ***mahā-mantra*** **and the** ***gāyatrī-mantra*****. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*pākāspade lavana-tīkṣṇa-rasādi-gandhaiḥ*  
*dugdhānna-vyañjana-phalādi-su-pakva-bhārtā*  
*kṣīrādi-rāsa-rasanāmṛta-pūrṇa-dāyī*  
*sevām pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ‘smi*

**”In the kitchen he masterfully cooked milk products, grains, vegetables, fruits and other ingredients, flavoring them with salt and spices such as chili. He thus served full plates of tasteful preparations, beginning with sweet rice, which were truly nectar for the tongue. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*āpanna-śasya-vīṭapī-śruti-sāra-śāstraṁ*  
*bhāṣāntareṇa bahu-deśa-vibhakti-kārtā*  
*kṛṣṇasya hṛt-vivaraṇaṁ para-duḥkha-duḥkhī*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ’smi*

**”He translated** ***Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*****, which is the essence of the tree of Vedic literature, and through his disciples he distributed it throughout many countries. Thus he whose only sorrow lies in the sorrows of others, uncovered their hearts to reveal the presence of Kṛṣṇa. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*māyā-vimūḍha-kali-bhañjana-deva-gātā*  
*kalyāṇa-dṛṣṭi-puruṣottama-dāna-pātram*  
*tīvreṇa bhakty-abhilaṣeṇa ku-deśa-pānthaḥ*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ‘smi*

**”His singing of Kṛṣṇa’s glories illusioned the illusory energy and disrupted Kali’s plans for disruption. By his earnest devotional hankering, he invoked the charitable glance of Lord Jagannātha and thus He also traveled to bless the degraded countries of the West. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service on me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*ānanda-sāgara-rasāyana-dhīra-mūrte*  
*śrī-rādhikā-madana-mohana-tṛpta-sphūrte*  
*tat-pāda-śiṣya-nayanātma-su-tuṣṭi-pūrte*  
*sevāṁ pradehi prabhupāda nu kiṅkaro ‘smi*

**”O Prabhupāda! Your form is filled with sobriety, yet you taste the elixir of the ocean of bliss. Your form is brilliantly manifest because you give pleasure to Śrī Śrī Rādhikā-Madana-Mohana, and you are completely filled with satisfaction by leading your disciples to Their lotus feet. O Prabhupāda! Please bestow your service unto me, for I am ready to accept your order.”**

*murāri-sevakānāṁ hi*  
*satataṁ dautya-sevane*  
*kṛpayāsmāsu namreṣu*  
*tava dhūliḥ sadāstu re*

**”O Śrīla Prabhupāda, kindly allow the dust of your lotus feet to perpetually fall upon us, who are the humble servants of Lord Murārī and are forever engaged in assisting your mission.”**

*Your servants at Murāri-sevaka Farm Community, Mulberry, Tennessee.*  
(offering composed by Jaya Balarāma dāsa brahmacārī)

## Newcastle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept this humble offering on the most wonderful day of your divine appearance. It is stated in the *Bhagavad-gītā* (7.14):

*mahātmānas tu māṁ pārtha*  
*daivīṁ prakṛtim āśritāḥ*  
*bhajanty ananya-manaso*  
*jñātvā bhūtādim avyayam*

**”O son of Pṛthā, those who are not deluded, the great souls, are under the protection of the divine nature. They are fully engaged in devotional service because they know Me as the Supreme Personality of Godhead, original and inexhaustible.**

”In this verse the description of the *mahātmā* is clearly given. The first sign of the *mahātmā* is that he is already situated in the divine nature. He is not under the control of material nature.”

This is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, fully ensconced on the transcendental platform of full devotional service to your Guru Mahārāja, Śrī Pañca-tattva and Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. We on the other hand, the *durātmās*, are struggling to see our way through the modes of material nature in order to serve your divine lotus feet, which are our only hope. Please look compassionately upon us in our various strivings to live by stepping in the imprints left by your footsteps. When a toddling child tries to follow a trail in wet sand of an adult’s making, often he may miss the footprint altogether, even though it is left large and clear. Due to his unsteady nature, he stumbles and totters. Nevertheless, he carries on trying to put his small feet in the grownup’s footprints. Similarly, we are trying to follow you divine trail of *bhakti-mārga*. Please take our hand and guide us along, making us strong. “We do not find the strength to carry on alone the *saṅkīrtana* of the holy name of Hari... We are simply ‘stumbling’ behind you shouting Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing us this far. We pray that you continue pulling us along in your wake. Thus, by your grace, we may one day also leave behind us the debilitating influence of t he material nature and come under the full protection of the divine nature.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to your appearance in this world.

*The servants of your servants in Newcastle, England*.

## New Gokula

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*ime jana-padāḥ suṛddhāḥ supakvausadhi-vīrudha*  
*vanādri-nady-udanvanto hy edhante tava vīkṣitair*

**”All these cities and villages are flourishing in all respects because the herbs and grains are in abundance, the trees are full of fruits, the rivers are flowing, the hills are full of minerals and the oceans full of wealth. And this is all due to Your glancing over them.” (*****Bhāg.*** **1.8.41)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to Your Divine Grace. As you can see when you drive into New Gokula, the vegetable patch is doing nicely, neatly mulched and maintained and producing the Deities’ requirements. Close by, half an acre of buds proclaim the farm’s first attempt at grain production; the following is the proud work of Gokula’s bullocks.

Arriving at the temple we see a quaint rammed earth structure, not grand, but pleasantly compatible with its first surroundings, glorified by a concerto of kookaburras, magpies and bellbirds.

Inside the temple, you mercifully reside on your Vyāsasana, facing the beautiful forms of Jagannātha, Balarāma and Subhadrā, Śrī Śrī Gaura Nitāia, and Śrī Śrī Rādhā Gokulānanda, whose beauty defies our earthly words. Around the temple, the lawns are mowed and the gardens are weeded and neat for your pleasure.

Across the right paddock the *brahmacārīs* are engaged in shifting the *gurukula* to its new location, two kilometers away in Millfield village, where we have tacit approval to develop this very dear project of yours. The *brahmacārīs* will now live in the old *gurukula* building, which has ample facility for about one dozen *brahmacārīs*, whose simple joyous life is the soul of New Gokula.

And appropriate to its central location, is the cow barn, where the sweet-faced Jersey mothers enjoy their hand milking and reciprocate with bounteous creamy milk for *rasagullas* and *sandesh*, *burfi* and sweet rice. This too is possible only by the gifts of Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as your aspiring servants, we are duty bound to work for the establishment of the “other half” of your work, although we are quite inadequate to comprehend the magnitude and gravity of your vision. To that end we are praying fervently on this special day for a dispensation of special mercy from your soft lotus feet.

*From all your servants at New Gokula Dhāma, Australia*.

## New Goloka

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We offer our humble obeisances to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, who is the dear disciple of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja.

On this auspicious occasion of your 98thappearance day celebration, we are very happy to take the dust of your lotus feet on our heads. We again and again offer our humble obeisances and take shelter of your divine lotus feet. Your lotus feet are very special, because they are smeared with the dust particles of Vṛndāvana, the land of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. The shelter of your lotus feet can fulfill all our desires. You are the powerful general of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* army. You have very kindly given us the most cherished treasure of love of Kṛṣṇa to relish and distribute, in the form of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana*, your books, your *vāṇī*, your personal *darśana*, and the most beautiful and merciful Deity forms of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. In spite of our total unworthiness, you have showered your mercy upon us and given us all these wonderful gifts for which even the greatest personalities of this universe are hankering. Therefore, we are eternally indebted to you. Please consider us as your eternal menial servants.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as far as the outside world is concerned, it is in great turmoil. In the name of progress and civilization, they are slaughtering innocent animals, opening bars and gambling casinos. Humans are wasting their lives away in animalistic passions. The general mass of people is suffering like anything. People of different communities, races and countries are fighting one another for land and resources. Fighting is going on in Bosnia, the former Soviet Union, Korea, Palestine, Rwanda, South Africa and many, many more places. The politicians are giving false promises of peace and prosperity. The scientists are busy creating a phantasmagoria. The *māyāvādīs* and atheists are tricking people away from God. Under these circumstances, your devotees are busy rescuing the innocent souls and bringing them back to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. In New Goloka Temple, your devotees are engaged in *hari-nāma kīrtana*, *prasāda* distribution, college preaching, book distribution, Deity worship, congregational preaching, etc. Devotees are strictly following the *sādhana* guidelines given by you. With our efforts on the devotional path, we only want to please you. We only pray that we will be able to execute whatever service you may desire for us. We pray that we can become worthy of spreading your fame and spreading the message you have given us to distribute. We only pray for your blessings to accomplish the mission of our lives.

We beg to remain you eternal servants,  
*The devotees of New Goloka, Hillsborough, North Carolina*.  
(Written by Viśvambhara dāsa Adhikārī)

## New Govardhana (Australia)

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the divine savior of this wretched Kali-yuga – you are Kṛṣṇa’s master artist.

You so skillfully paint your pure canvas – that unblemished statement of light and truth.

Carefully you sketched out the plan as ordained by your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa Himself.

When the basic draft was done, you came to engage the many in your masterpiece for all time.

Each soul, who gains your mercy, pure by nature, but so covered by ignorance, is extracted from the mud of material life, forcefully cleansed, purified to become part of your eternal work of art.

The color and hue from one pigment to another, are so vastly different, and yet bright, vibrant and fresh when allowed to partake in the master’s work.

Divinely blessed, each soul, like color, has its own uniqueness and brilliance clearly visible when taken by you and placed on this massive canvas.

With some colors you make a big splash and give prominence, others you use in small sections of the painting, others you combine in harmony together. So many colors are placed on the canvas, and you skill assures there are no clashes of color, only harmony. Some colors are placed with great vigor and broad-brush strokes and others are smoothly applied and gently caressed – the overall effect is fantastic, so rich, so pure – such a wonderful offering.

Your every stroke and application is done with so much attention to detail, precision and, most of all, love.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the divine savior of this wretched Kali-yuga – you are Kṛṣṇa’s master artist. You are revealing Kṛṣṇa to everyone – that is your work of art.

Please allow us to be part of your masterpiece, your wonderful work of love. We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for by your mercy, we have been picked up from the dull, dirty material pool and placed on your transcendental palette, cleansed by your loving hands to reveal our true color and used in anyway you please. We pray to never ever be removed from that superb masterpiece.

## New Govardhana (San Diego)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Śrīla Prabhupāda dayā karaha vicāra*  
*vicāra karile citte pābe camatkāra*

Anyone interested in logic and argument should apply them to the mercy of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda! If they do so they will find it strikingly wonderful.

For more lives than anyone can count, we have  
worked hard at becoming the most fallen of souls,  
Our hallmarks, our pride: ignorance, misfortune, and sin.  
We have majored in forgetting Kṛṣṇa, doing extra coursework  
in denying our eternal relationship with the Lord.  
Our own bodies, born and bred in Kali-yuga,  
serve as our diplomats: Full Number One,  
devoid of any grace.

But Lord Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Thief;  
He has sent Your Divine Grace, His personal envoy,  
to show the folly of our self-imposed exile.  
You have changed our lives by demonstrating  
pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa, conceivable to us only  
by Your Divine Grace.  
The matchless gift, which  
immediately relieves all material distress,  
opens the way to the best of fortune,  
bestows transcendental happiness,  
is rarely achieved,  
transcends even liberation,  
and is the only way to attract Kṛṣṇa,  
is available only at the lotus feet  
of the pure spiritual master.

Anyone who wants any happiness must learn  
*bhakti* from a bona fide *guru,*  
making his word one’s life and soul.  
Lord Caitanya shows this as the root  
of the devotional creeper.  
We can attain love for Kṛṣṇa  
only by the spiritual master’s  
divine grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the supreme professor  
of the science of devotional service.  
Anyone who claims to know anything about Kṛṣṇa  
must have heard it from you.  
Any goodness found in the word in this age of anxiety and discord, is strictly due to your taking the word of your spiritual master  
as life and soul, and showering us with  
your causeless mercy.

The task of preaching is our legacy;  
you left you mission incomplete  
so we could spend our lives pursuing its fulfillment.  
We are your limbs in this dark world, and  
apart from you, our lives have no meaning;  
apart from Your Divine Grace –  
your causeless mercy – we have no life.

As we approach the Śrīla Prabhupāda Centennial year,  
your helpless children – your “boys and girls”  
(though boys and girls, perhaps, only in your eyes anymore),  
those who came in the years of struggle,  
the new counterculture kids who remind us  
of ourselves not so long ago,  
and the world’s great hope, the *gurukula* alumni,  
who show us all how to remember Kṛṣṇa  
everywhere and when –  
pray to dedicate our work, words, and thoughts  
to establishing your reputation as the universal teacher of pure devotion  
so all may be attracted to your lotus feet  
and receive your causeless mercy.

Just as we get water from the earth by digging,  
we hope to get one more drop of your mercy  
by our service to your great mission.  
That drop of mercy, we hope will yield  
humility, forbearance, compassion,  
and freedom from personal ambition.  
Only then will be fit to free  
all fallen souls of Kali-yuga  
from the ignorance and misfortune they so prize  
and share the great treasure of  
Your Divine Grace.

*Your servants at New Govardhana Dhāma, San Diego, California*.  
(written by Babhru dāsa)

## New Jagannātha Purī

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

”The lotus feet of our spiritual master are the only way by which we can attain pure devotional service. I bow to his lotus feet with great awe and reverence. By his grace one can cross the ocean of material suffering and obtain the mercy of Kṛṣṇa.”

It is only through your ambrosial instructions that we can understand the message of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi. Their teachings are for us in this age of Kali-yuga and we don’t know how to implement them. We are constantly bickering about petty issues that divide our community. As you Centennial approaches nearer and nearer, we are being forced to realize that if we have any love for you, we must work together to glorify Your Divine Grace. Your instructions are enlivening and we need to always keep them in the forefront of our thoughts. This is our only salvation. We beg to remain at your lotus feet, the purifying residence of the fallen.

”My only wish is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from his lotus mouth. Attachment to his lotus feet is the perfection that fulfills all desires.”

You are *jagat-guru* – the teacher of the world. We know nothing. You are here to teach us about Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda and Their love for us, but we continue to be dull-headed and make mistakes. You are teaching us that our service to the Divine Couple and to you is what will make our lives successful. It is only through your grace that we continue to attempt to serve Them. We pray that you will continue to instruct us about devotional service.

”He opens my darkened eyes and fills my heart with transcendental knowledge. He is my lord birth after birth. From him ecstatic *premā* emanates; by him ignorance is destroyed. The Vedic scriptures sing of his character.”

If we remain at your lotus feet, we cannot but hear your delectable words. The knots of our false egos that tie our hearts will be cut by the pure blade of your voice. You are the hope of the world, the hope of our lives. Please help us to serve you.

”Our spiritual master is the ocean of mercy, the friend of the poor, and the lord and master of devotees. O master! Be merciful unto me. Give me the shade of your lotus feet. Your fame is spread all over the three worlds.”

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are peerless, unrivaled in the three worlds. You have come to us and given us what we need the most – the love of Lord Jagannātha. We pray that you will continue to let us receive the glowing smiles of Lord Jagannātha, Lady Subhadrā and Lord Baladeva at New Jagannātha Purī. Please accept our service and continue to inspire us. Our only hope is that what service we do will be pleasing to you.

We beg to remain perpetual servants,  
*Your aspiring devotees at New Jagannātha Purī, Berkeley, California*.  
(by Satya devī dāsī)

## New Māyāpura

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*brahmāṇḍa brahmite kona bhāgyavān jīva*  
*guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja*

”According to their *karma*, all living entities are wandering throughout the entire universe. Some of them are being elevated to the upper planetary systems, and some are going down into the lower planetary systems. Out of many millions of wandering living entities, one who is very fortunate gets an opportunity to associate with a bona fide spiritual master by the grace of Kṛṣṇa. By the mercy of both Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master, such a person receives the seed of the creeper of devotional service.” (*Cc. Madhya* 19.151)

Due to previous karma, one is forced to accept a body in a country, in a certain area, in a family, belonging to a specific *varṇa*, enjoying certain opulence, and conditioned in a way particular to the country where one lives. In this way, we can see that all over the world different countries have their own traditions, habits and customs.

Some particularities of the French conditioning are:  
a natural tendency for philosophy (and speculation);  
an unsubmissive attitude towards leadership;  
a revolutionary spirit.

Being all parts and parcels of Kṛṣṇa, these particularities are also there in the Supreme Personality of Godhead in an absolute way. In Caitanya-*līlā*, we can see that they have been manifested on some occasions:

Lord Caitanya has shown that He was the greatest philosopher when He discussed with Sarvabhauma Bhaṭṭācārya.  
He organized civil disobedience when Chand Kazi forbade the *hari-nāma* to go on.  
His congregational chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa is the greatest revolution in the history of religion and has created a great disturbance in the minds of so-called spiritualists.

Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as taught by Lord Caitanya, has come down through the *guru-paramparā* and was brought to the Western shores by His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda. Śrīla Prabhupāda’s coming to the West was not done whimsically. We find the following verse in the *Bhāgavata-mahātmya*:

*idaṁ sthānaṁ paritjyajya*  
*vedeṣāṁ gamyate mayā*

*Bhakti* personified speaks to Śrīla Nārada Muni: ”I will leave this country (India) and go to the West.” We understand that Śrīla Prabhupāda is that personality. He brought devotion (*bhakti*) to the West where she is waiting for her appropriate reception.

The spiritual world is not void. On the contrary, it is full of variegatedness. And the process by which we can go back to Godhead is not a dry process of negation. Śrī Kṛṣṇa teaches us in the *Bhagavad-gītā*:

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*anāśritaḥ karma-phalaṁ*  
*kāryaṁ karma karoti yaḥ*  
*sa sannyāsi ca yogī ca*  
*na niragnir na cākriyaḥ*

**”The Supreme Personality of Godhead said: One who is unattached to the fruits of his work and who works as he is obliged is in the renounced order of life, and he is the true mystic, not he who lights no fire and performs no work.” (*****Bg.*** **6.1)**

The process for advancing in spiritual life is an active process. But being conditioned due to our previous *karma*, how can we act spiritually, being under the influence of the *guṇas*? Śrīla Prabhupāda has shown us practically how to dovetail our natural tendencies in the service of the Lord.

By studying the wonderful philosophy left by Śrīla Prabhupāda, our philosophical tendency will become satisfied and the propensity to speculate will stop in front of the staunch arguments Śrīla Prabhupāda exposes.  
The tendency to disobey is natural when the leaders are cheaters who create a soul-killing civilization. With the knowledge give by Prabhupāda, and his practical exemplary life, let us disobey the demands of the mind controlled by the gross senses that lead us to another conditioned life, and let us surrender fully to His instructions that free us from the cycle of birth and death.  
Let us make a big revolution in France, a spiritual revolution that will bring spiritually inclined leaders to the head of the country, rather than people lower than *śūdras* who are blind men leading the country towards hell.

If we don’t use these natural tendencies in Kṛṣṇa’s service, but use them in internal politics, we shall become employees of māyā; our progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness will be slowed down, and the preaching in France will be stopped.

Lord Caitanya predicted:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi-grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

**”In every town and village, the chanting of My name will be heard.” (*****Cc. ādi*** **17.203)**

Lord Caitanya wanted His name to be broadcast all over the world. Śrīla Prabhupāda is the bona fide representative of Lord Caitanya. By *guru-paramparā*, we accept this philosophy. To celebrate this Vyāsa-pūjā means to accept this *paramparā* system, to stop this speculative tendency and to completely surrender to the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

To make this revolution a success, Śrīla Prabhupāda gave the recipe: chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, take *mahā-prasāda*, work cooperatively with our Godbrothers and serve Kṛṣṇa and His devotees.

On this holy Vyāsa-pūjā day, let us offer Śrīla Prabhupāda our obeisances and glorify his name and instructions.

We thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for sending us so many great souls to help the devotees in France use their revolutionary spirit in spreading Kṛṣṇa’s glories and making a spiritual revolution. We humbly pray that you will continue to shower us with your mercy so that we can make our lives successful by your grace.

New Māyāpura, France  
(by Pītavāsa dāsa Adhikārī)

## New Nandagrāma

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

A common characteristic of all great men is their far-reaching vision. If vision is the measuring stick to a man’s greatness then even the most lowly simpleton can understand something of your unique stature among the giants of history. Even the greatest among your spiritual contemporaries could not appreciate the depth of your vision. Those to whom you revealed your vision considered you to be well meaning but overly optimistic. However, your vision could penetrate the obstacles obstructing the vision of lesser men. You were uniquely inspired by the instructions of your *guru mahārāja*. With unflinching determination you took those instructions as your life and soul, confident that by adhering to those instructions you would invoke the mercy of Lord Caitanya, by whose desire blind men can see and lame men can walk. What great vision you had!

Your unprecedented vision contained a house for the whole world to live in. Even the great Maya Dānava, architect by appointment to the gods of the universe, could not have envisaged such a house. Expert as he may be, he can only manipulate the subtle and gross elements, such as rare jewels, rare metals, gold and the use of mystic *mantras*. None of these ingredients could build the house you had in mind. The ingredients you required were not available at any material marketplace anywhere in the universe, nor could they be summoned by mystic *mantra*. You required the rarest of all ingredients, full and voluntary surrender to *guru* and God. What great vision you had!

Beyond the darkest and most forbidding regions of material existence these ingredients lay, guarded by very ancient and powerful demons in the forms of lust, avarice, greed and the desire to be supreme. The awesome power of these demons in union devoured anything and anyone who dared challenge their reign. The Western regions of the world which you decided to explore were guarded by especially large and fierce demons who had ruled supreme for thousands of years. What great vision you had!

The rest is glorious history, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda. With the tools and weapons of all encompassing compassion, love, mercy and fixed determination you were able to venture and succeed in what others thought impossible. The demons did indeed rear their ugly heads, only to have them chopped off by your transcendental weapons. You were able to extract some raw elements and begin construction. Now, that house in the form of ISKCON stands as a testament to your greatness. A unique mansion whose foundations – consisting of strict adherence to your instruction, the chanting of at least sixteen rounds daily, reading your books with devotional scrutiny, lack of personal ambition, cooperation and determination to succeed – hold up the four main walls of no intoxication, no gambling, no meat-eating and no illicit sex-life, which in turn support the roof of the house which shields the occupants from harsh external elements, keeping them safe and protected. What great vision you had!

You are the greatest benefactor whose benevolence has provided the spiritually destitute people of the world a safe haven and eternal hope. As the guests occupying one particular room of your transcendental mansion, we pray that we may never forget our previous helplessness and most fallen condition and never become complacent nor take for granted the facility you have kindly provided for us. We pray that we may keep our room spotlessly clean and tidy as though you may at any moment, as the master of the house, decide to come in unannounced. We pray that our hearts do not harden to exclude others who knock on our door, seeking shelter from the onslaught of material existence, remembering well how we ourselves were saved. We pray that the foundation of your great house remain strong lest our walls develop cracks and our roof leaks. We pray dear Śrīla Prabhupāda for your eternal mercy and guidance without which we would be ruined. Countless *daṇḍavats* at your divine lotus feet which tirelessly traversed this fortunate planet.

We pray to remain always the servants of your servants.

*Your children and grandchildren of the New Nandagrāma Community, Australia*  
(by Yugala-kiśora dāsa)

## New Ramaṇa-reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ‘ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtam aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**”O my Lord! Transcendental poets and experts in spiritual science could not fully express their indebtedness to You, even if they were endowed with the prolonged lifetime of Brahmā, for You appear in two features – externally as the** ***ācārya*** **and internally as the Supersoul – to deliver the embodied living being by directing him how to come to You.” (*****Bhāg*****. 11.29.6)**

In this regard, all of your disciples, grand disciples and followers here in the New Ramaṇa-reti community would like to acknowledge our indebtedness to you for all that you have done for us and continue to do by way of lighting up the path back to home back to Godhead. May you be pleased with our attempted efforts as a symptom of our underlying sincerity and bestow upon us the real desire to commit ourselves cent percent to the execution of your instructions.

*cakhu-dāan dilo jei, janme janme prabhu sei,*  
*divya-jñān hṛde prokaśito*

**”He opens my darkened eyes and fills my heart with transcendental knowledge. He is my Lord birth after birth.”**

Begging to remain always engaged in your service.

*Your loving servants at New Ramaṇa-reti, Alachua, Florida*.

## New Ramaṇa-reti Dhāma

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most worthless *daṇḍavats* in the dust of your lotus feet again and again. We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet that have traveled across the world, from country to country, city to city, town to town, and village to village. We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which walked through mud and rubble to oversee the construction of the grandest temples in ISKCON. You made sure that no stone was left uncovered from securing land to acquiring materials to building in the highest of standards.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which have walked through hundreds of ISKCON centers which you so lovingly established. With your mercy, temples, centers, and farm communities are on each and every continent around the world.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which walked into the hearts of thousands upon thousands of disciples and grand disciples. You have empowered all of us to make humble attempts to continue serving your mission.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which have stood at numerous altars to perform grand *āratis* to Their Lordships. You showed us neophyte devotees how to worship Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Lady Subhadrā.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which have brought the most beautiful Deities for millions of people to worship.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which walked across cold floors at 2 a.m. in the morning to translate and write books for the benefit of all mankind.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which personally stood to distribute literature from *Back to Godheads* to *Bhagavad-gītās*. By your example, you empowered us to distribute millions upon millions of books.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which walked before carts down the largest boulevards and thorough ways in major cities around the world. You showed the world the grandest festivals.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which patiently stood in front of hot stoves to cook for Their Lordships. Through your example thousands of offerings are being prepared for Their Lordships every day around the world.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which stood by while distributing *prasādam* to the devotees in the early days. You have allowed us to distribute over one billion plates of *prasādam* around the world.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which danced in ecstasy for Their Lordships. Your chanting and dancing has kept us dancing forever.

We pay our *daṇḍavats* to your lotus feet which came to Toronto and blessed the local devotees.

Please keep us in the shelter of your lotus feet for eternity. All glories to your lotus feet!

Your worthless servants at New Ramaṇa-reti Dhāma, Toronto, Canada.  
(written by Śubhavilāsa dāsa)

## New Talavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you. May your devotional service expand unlimitedly, and may we always be your humble disciples. Please forgive our innumerable faults. Chastise us so that our actions may be more pleasing to you.

You are always available via your instructions and teachings. Your work and disciples are manifestations of yourself. As a major force in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* party, your service is tremendous. While physically among us, it was as if you were planting an orchard. Now that orchard is growing as the Pañca-tattva watches, waters, engineers, and cultivates it. Thus we understand we are simply your instruments for furthering the Lord’s *saṅkīrtana-jajña*.

The eternal and individual nature of the soul and Supersoul are the basis of your transcendental teachings. We should never doubt your authoritative presentations as absolute fact, and our lives should be modeled on this premise. Therefore, we speak, work, and pray in the knowledge that you will appreciate our activities.

Although we are very insignificant, still we long for some small recognition from you to reassure ourselves. Navigating your temple, farm, and devotees through this dangerous ocean of Kali is worrisome and full of menacing obstacles. Thus we follow your basic instructions and depend on your mercy. Please inspire us to pull through in Kṛṣṇa consciousness for your full satisfaction. Help us to operate and drive your *saṅkīrtana* movement forward. May we forever taste the rich mellows of loving devotional service to you. We therefore plead with you to please accept our humble service in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*Your servants at New Talavana Yātrā Carrier, Mississippi*.

## New Varsānā

*mukaṁ karoti vācālaṁ paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**”By the mercy of the spiritual master, a lame man can cross over mountains, and a dumb man can compose eloquent poetry.”**

To our dearmost master and friend, Śrīla Prabhupāda, of oceanic greatness:

You’re of oceanic greatness,  
Though you feel yourself small.  
Attached to your *guru*,  
You give Kṛṣṇa to all.

Full faith in Kṛṣṇa’s names;  
You know They’re both the same.  
Why don’t we follow  
And achieve eternal gain?

Life’s meant for knowing who the Lord is,  
But through His servant only His mercy He’ll give.  
Prabhupāda, you showed how to serve constantly,  
Pleasing your Kṛṣṇa with every thought, word and deed.

You’re dependent on Him (that’s why we feel safe),  
Never thinking you’re the doer, or Lord of the place.  
You know the Lord well, bosom buddies always been:  
Seeing everything as His, you’ve not once thought of sin.  
Saving the world with your love for the Lord,  
You went through hell just to give us His word!

Faith beyond measure, so you could do more  
Than men could imagine, above *māyā* to soar.  
”Old and alone” in the West’s Big Rotten Apple,  
You unleashed the power with *māyā* to grapple.  
A power so great, that scholars would see  
That from humble beginnings, you’d change history.

We maddened souls must the same battle fight,  
But haven’t such faith to give us the might.  
The fortress of *māyā*, lust in our hearts;  
Our only hope, Gurudeva, your power impart.

The words in your books you gave us for that.  
You’re still with us now: simply read and it’s fact!  
You spoke only truth, pure power in sound.  
If we just hear and share it, one day we’ll come ‘round  
Back to your place at Śyāma’s lotus feet  
Together one day again we will meet.

You fanned the spark of pure love that’s in all,  
Then, more, you trusted us to take up the call.  
Now you’re waiting for us to trust one another:  
Cooperation, the key, to live as a sister and brother.  
Then surely you’ll be happy with our childish attempts  
To give up our envy, greed, anger, contempt.

You’ve built the house for the world family,  
But what’s in the home if its not charity?  
We can be your children – you’re our father, all.  
But we’ll have to forgive, and let the pride fall!  
You’ve shown us the way, humility’s the jewel.  
We’ll win you pleasure by that golden rule.

Prabhupāda, please help us to please you, and then  
We’re sure to be with you, back home again.

All glories to your pure love for Śrī Kṛṣṇa and for us all, unworthy, yet hopeful, servants (of your servants) at New Varsānā, New Zealand  
(by ātmānanda dāsa)

## New Vrajamaṇḍala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances prostrated in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

When we hear about your big achievements in the world expansion of the saṅkīrtana movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and consider the many transcendental qualities that you have exhibited in your devotional service, we can only be inspired and feel attracted to your marvelous personality. You are unique and special, someone who could not be easily found in this world.

It is not rare to find rich, strong, intelligent persons, materially speaking, but to get to know someone who is totally dedicated to satisfying the desire of the Supreme Lord, without any hesitation of confusion, with his heart completely pure, overflowing with the desire to glorify Kṛṣṇa, and always eager to bless all the living entities with the happiness of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, is something totally extraordinary.

You created a powerful movement, disciplined and revolutionary, disposed to confront the darkness of this miserable age of Kali; you are the strength and inspiration that maintain us working in this army of fighters. If it was not for your impeccable life and teachings, who would decide to abandon the abominable and stinking bad habits that kept us trapped on all sides? Who would dare to confront the innumerable illusions that trap the consciousness of the spirit soul? Who would have the courage to say the truth in a world where nobody wants to listen? You are the endless source of any achievement that we could never reach. Without you life would not have any value and would be wasted in a vain search for the phantasmagoria of material pleasure. It was only you, who from the beginning, created faith where there was only hopelessness and emptiness. You were the one who organized the life of those people who were immersed in confusion and whims. You were the one who made us understand with your brilliance how we were being poisoned and were rotting in the nightmare of material life.

So in so many ways you gave us in abundance the nectar of immortality: Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Even so, despite all the miracles that you performed on us, we continue feeling the tendency to forget Kṛṣṇa and to try to enjoy the mundane illusion. However, despite all, we are convinced of the power and infallibility of your instructions, and we can see that those who are intensely attached to your instructions are not growing weak in their efforts to please you. They are obtaining the result of their devotional service and, at the same time, they are helping those who are following them to be successful.

The clear conclusion is that our only duty and interest is to take full shelter of your sacred lotus feet and in others who are following faithfully in your footsteps.

In this way, we take advantage of the day of your advent to ask you if you could help us to get free of all the confusion that overwhelms us and bless us, giving us the intelligence that we need to expertly perform our devotional service and not waste time and energy in activities that do not help us increase our love for Kṛṣṇa. Please, smash our pride and false ego and make us abandon our repeatedly offensive attitudes.

We offer our obeisances once again unto your lotus feet, and we ask for your full protection from the attacks of *māyā*.

*Your poor servants from New Vrajamaṇḍala, Guadalajara, Spain*

## New York (26 Second Avenue)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. All glories to your representatives who are helping us to carry on your mission.

We are so fortunate to be sitting here at 26 2ndAvenue, the place where it all began, the place where you first came and introduced so many to the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa. Out of compassion, you left Vṛndāvana on the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī and, being empowered by Kṛṣṇa, you transported Vṛndāvana consciousness to the Lower East Side. Your heart being filled with Mahāprabhu’s mercy, you then began to inundate the world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we remember you here in this intimate setting, a simple servant of anyone who came through the front door. You personally cooked, cleaned, sat on a plain straw mat (on an equal level with everyone else) and performed your *bhajan*. Being the epitome of the servant of the servant – giving yourself fully to Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s mission – you embraced even difficult circumstances when they seemed favorable to its execution.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we feel your presence today at this storefront by trying to take up your simple, humble example and by opening our arms to those who are seeking your shelter. We see the recent resurrection of this storefront as your special mercy on us. Since you have left this world, we have, sometimes, as an institution, lost sight of this simple essence. This *tīrtha* is giving us the opportunity to remind and introduce many devotees to the humble roots of this movement. By your mercy we wish to maintain this center as the hub of ISKCON where all types of devotees can come and engage in your glorification. We practically experience that all discrepancies are reconciled when your presence is felt strongly. Please bless us that we may continue to host all of your dear devotees. Please continue to guide us and use us as your instruments. Your mercy is all that we are made of.

*Your servants at 26 2ndAvenue, New York City.*

## New York (Brooklyn)

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

All glories to your transcendental appearance day and Vyāsa-pūjā celebration. This day is always the sweetest and most wonderful because of the mellow taste of hearing so many sincere homages offered with love and devotion for your pleasure at your transcendental lotus feet.

In 1975 on a walk in Atlanta with a large platoon of book distribution, you were asked by a devotee \[Nirantara\], “Śrīla Prabhupāda, what pleases you the most?”

And you responded, perhaps much to the surprise of some of the devotees, “to see that my disciple is learning to love Kṛṣṇa.”

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**”By the grace of the spiritual master, one receives the benediction of Kṛṣṇa. Without the grace of the spiritual master, one cannot make any advancement. Therefore, I should always remember and praise the spiritual master. At least three times a day, I should offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of my spiritual master.”**

So we are certain, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that what pleases Lord Kṛṣṇa the most is to see that His aspiring servants on the path of *bhakti-yoga* are learning to love His pure, unconditional devotee, His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami, Śrīla Prabhupāda *kī jaya*.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you possess all the transcendental qualities of the topmost pure devotee of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, the effects of your mercy on the hearts of your sincere, dedicated servants and worshipers, admirers by the millions, is like that of the full moon arousing the vast oceans.

Your love for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa qualified Your Divine Grace as the topmost *ācārya*, master, friend, and supreme servant of all of us fortunate enough to recognize your glorious attributes. Your feelings for the devotees went far beyond the external etiquette of mundane benevolence. You love Kṛṣṇa, and you love His devotees and the effects of that love has given us the strength to continue serving ISKCON in an attempt to fulfill your desires and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world.

When your transcendental mercy shines in a disciple’s heart, we see wonderful accomplishments. So many books printed and distributed, so many beautiful temples constructed for the long-term propagation of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, so many unfortunate conditioned *jīvas* receiving the mercy of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Your Divine Grace, elevating them to the topmost platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Benedicted by your mercy, the world becomes full of good fortune, an oasis in the desert, a soothing, shining light to lead us out of the dark cave. When you were physically present, every devotee who had the good fortune of meeting you even for a fraction of a moment became a recipient of your mercy – they realized your all-powerful, transcendental loving potencies, and they were willing and very eager to serve Your Divine Grace forever.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Mādhya-Līlā* 22.54, it is stated:

*’sādhu-saṅga’, ‘sādhu-saṅga’ – sarva-śāstre kaya*  
*lava-mātra sādhu-saṅge sarva-siddhi haya*

**”The verdict of all the revealed scriptures is that by even a moment’s association with a pure devotee, one can attain all success.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have never met anyone past, present, or future who affected our hearts as Your Divine Grace has. Your transcendental presence is more powerful than the conditions set upon us by material nature. Grown men and women were impelled from within their hearts to fall prostrate on the floor just by having your physical *darśan*.

Making arrangements for your arrival at any one of your numerous temples, the devotees could work overtime and double overtime, inspired and exhilarated by thoughts of your impeding arrival and association. Sometimes a senior devotee who had close contact with you would inspire the book distributors by telling them that their book scores would be read to you the next morning, and they would run from person to person from morning to night giving your mercy, your transcendental books, those brilliant, purifying purports just so you would know that they gave the whole day with no energy to spare simply for your pleasure, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Adults and children alike standing before you during your *guru-pūjā*, tears in their eyes overwhelmed with emotions, voices choked, they will never forget you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Seeing so many exhibitions of transcendental potency, we begin to wonder, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to know, what is that you’ve got, what is your secret, how do you keep them all so happy, so friendly, peaceful and kind? Is it because of your vast scholarship that we became attracted, or was it your managerial expertise? Perhaps it is that charming personality, that big heart-warming smile. It lights up the whole room and makes everyone respond in kind. But then again maybe it’s the regal manner with which you carry your good self or the absolute conviction in your voice when you defeat a *māyāvādī* with Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy, or the impression that comes through loud and clear that you’ve realized all transcendental absolute truths that you propound in your relentless preaching work.

All of these glorious attributes of yours considered, if we look very close, we will find a few more qualities that although very simple and sublime, really convinced us and captured us as your loyal servants forever.In *Bhagavad-gīṭā* 12.13-14 it is stated;

*adveṣṭā sarva-bhūtānāṁ*  
*maitraḥ karuṇa eva ca*  
*nirmamo nirahaṅkāraḥ*  
*sama-duḥkha-sukhaḥ kṣamī*

*santuṣṭaḥ satataṁ yogī*  
*yatātmā dṛḍha-niścayaḥ*  
*mayy arpita-mano-buddhir*  
*yo mad-bhaktaḥ sa me priyaḥ*

**”One who is not envious but is a kind friend to all living entities, who does not think himself a proprietor and is free from false ego, who is equal in both happiness and distress, who is tolerant, always satisfied, self-controlled, and engaged in devotional service with determination, his mind and intelligence fixed in Me – such a devotee of Mine is very dear to Me.”**

Your transcendental qualifications are mentioned in *Bhagavad-gītā*, and other places in the scriptures, and because we all to some degree or another realize that you are a confidential associate of Lord Kṛṣṇa. We are always very eager to serve Your Divine Grace, hear and follow your instructions, and assist you to push on this worldwide Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Everyone of us is your *śikṣā* disciple; you shall always be the *guru* and ever well-wishing friend of every member of ISKCON in this specific way: teaching all of us to love Śrī Kṛṣṇa and all of His devotees by the perfect Kṛṣṇa conscious teachings in Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s transcendental books, which Your Divine Grace so nicely authored for His pleasure and for the benefit of all mankind.

We want to thank you very much for saving us from the bondage of this material world/prison house and uplifting us to the platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, the topmost *yoga* system, and we fervently pray at your glorious lotus feet:

*he guro jñāna-da-dina-bandho*  
*svānanda-dātā karuṇaika-sindho*  
*vṛndāvanāsīna hitāvatāra*  
*prasīda rādhā-praṇāya-pracāra*

**”O spiritual master, O giver of divine wisdom, O friend of fallen, you are the giver of your own bliss and you are the only ocean of mercy. Although dwelling in Vṛndāvana, you have descended for the welfare of fallen souls like myself, and you are preaching the divine love of Rādhā for Kṛṣṇa. Please be kind upon me.”**

*We beg to remain your servants forever*,  
ISKCON Brooklyn, New York City.

## New Zealand

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble offering at your divine lotus feet. All glories to you on this auspicious day of your appearance.

**The Divine Gift**

You gave us a house in which the whole world could live, and a family of loving brothers, sisters and friends – all wonderful Vaiṣṇavas.

You gave us the ultimate survival kit for this dark and cold age of Kali, a medicine so potent that even stone hearts melt – *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana kī jaya!*

You gave us volumes of divine instruction in the form of books you translated, and by so doing you are protecting us for the next 10,000 years from impersonalistic speculation and materialistic contamination, so prevalent in the modern age.

You gave us Deities and *prasādam*, temples and the morning program, you gave us the GBC and *guru*, Māyāpura and Vṛndāvana. Thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Here in Wellington, New Zealand, we, your grand disciples are endeavoring to maintain the small facility mercifully bestowed upon the conditioned souls in this city by Your Divine Grace, and through your disciple Śrīla Prabhaviṣṇu Svāmī, our beloved Guru Mahārāja, we are gaining great strength and inspiration to expand the preaching more and more.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave your very life, so that all conditioned souls may again realize their eternal life in union with the supremely lovable person, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Begging for an ounce of your mercy and blessings, we are  
*Your most insignificant servants in the Wellington Yātrā, New Zealand*.

## Nitāi Navadvīpacandra Mandira

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, merciful pure devotee of the Lord.

So many times we have asked ourselves, “What was the secret of your success? What made you so successful in preaching to the *mlecchas* and *yavanas*? What makes your success go on, even after your departure from our mundane vision?” Many answers are given. Some said Your Divine Grace was a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra*. Some said you were one of Lord Caitanya’s associates. You personally told us that the secret of success was in your consistent, unalloyed devotion to your Guru Mahārāja. Here, in this *śloka* by Pṛthu Mahārāja, we of course found the truth: Your Divine Grace has contact with the “mercy particles that are strewn over the lotus feet of the Lord.” You write in the purport: “As soon as the sleeping living entity hears the powerful voice emanating from the mouth of a pure devotee, he immediately remembers his eternal relationship with the Lord, although up until that moment, he had forgotten everything.”

Up until we had met Your Divine Grace, up until we had heard your powerful voice and read your powerful translations and purports, we were forgetful of everything: Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya were so distant. We had absolutely no idea! Now, because we heard Your Divine Grace, we are starting to remember: “Yes, I am the eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa. Let me strive for going back home, back to Godhead.”

Let us glorify Your Divine Grace with selected words, which you so mercifully left us to contemplate upon:

”My dear highly glorified Lord, if one, in the association of pure devotees, hears even once the glories of Your activities, he does not, unless he is nothing but an animal, give up the association of devotees, for no intelligent person would be careless as to leave their association.
