The perfection of chanting and hearing about Your glories was accepted even by the goddess of fortune, who desired to hear of Your unlimited activities and transcendental glories.” (*Bhāg*. 4.20.26)

*Your ever-indebted servants and devout listeners from ISKCON, Nitāi Navadvīpacandra Mandir, Czech Republic.*  
(written by Jaya Gurudeva dāsa)

## Nuevo Goloka Vṛndāvana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,All glories to Śrī Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!  
All glories to Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara!  
All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Svāmī Prabhupāda!

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**”I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken refuge at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

On this day of your merciful appearance in this material world, we pray for you to accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we want to worship you as our real lord and master. As we approach your Centennial, it has given us clearer goals in this agricultural community project. Among all of our goals, the construction of a new temple for Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara takes the top priority, because this will enable us to develop a strong “Prabhupāda consciousness.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you, your life example and your matchless books are the inspiration in our lives, the foundation for which to build a solid Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Every disciple of yours brings us reminiscences of some aspect of your multifaceted personality: some remind us of the perfect way you solidly presented the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Some of your disciples have been able to capture a drop of your purity and simplicity, which has given them the attraction for Deity worship, writing, publishing and the distribution of Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy. You have descended from the spiritual world, empowered by Kṛṣṇa to do the impossible, that is to spread the message of Lord Caitanya all around the world. This you did in record time – only twelve years!

Vaiṣṇavas were wondering: “Who will finally come to fulfill Śrī Caitanya’s sacred order to distribute His holy names in every town and village? When will arrive that person that will please the desire of the previous *ācāryas*, of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura? When will the day come, when we will see that Vaiṣṇava with the power of devotion to bring others to the lotus feet of the Lord?”

Now it is known by the causeless mercy of Your Divine Grace, *hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana* has reached the most remote corners of this planet, which are resounding with the powerful sound of your *bṛhat-mṛdaṅga*, the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust. We have read from your books, that the secret of success for the disciple is the staunch faith in the words of spiritual master. In this way ISKCON is developing (and will develop even more) by neglecting the strong opposition of the atheistic materialists, and by following the principles established by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and by Your Divine Grace.

On the capital’s buses, the “*saṅkīrtaneros*” chant your transcendental names through their megaphones, presenting every day: “Ladies and gentlemen, we are introducing you to a new book of A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda...”

Once, we were doing *hari-nāma* at a nearby village, Paraiso. The kīrtana was very beautiful and intense. Many young people were dancing and singing with us (which was rather unusual in these conservative towns). One of the boys was practically in ecstasy, trying to shout along with us (maybe a little incorrectly): “Nitāi-Gaurāṅga! Nitāi Gaurāṅga!” When we suddenly changed the song, the boy, jumping very high, cried out: “Sing the one I know: Nitāi-Gaurāṅga!” In this way, we can see your mercy continuing even to this day.

What a pity, we are so unfortunate that we arrived too late, we were unable to serve your *vapu*, you physical form, as many of your disciples had, by serving you *prasāda*, massaging your transcendental body, or sending you a letter (knowing that very soon you would reply, “your ever well-wisher”). In your books you say that your *vapu* is not so important. To realize this, and to not feel separated from you, we have made a vow of putting forever on our heads your *vāṇī*, your divine instructions. Forever. We have the firm conviction that we will feel your full presence.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray for your mercy to be able to remember that the service to your devotees and the Vaiṣṇavas’ association are the highest benediction in life.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on your Vyāsa-pūjā day!

Your humble servants at Nueva Goloka Vṛndāvana, Costa Rica.

## Nuremberg

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept my most humble obeisances thrown in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

In your *gṛhastha* life you were a pharmacist administering lotions and potions for material ailments. Historians may even think of you like that: “a retired pharmacist from Calcutta.” In truth you never retired. You shifted the emphasis from physical afflictions to infections of the soul. From lambago, arthritis and gout to lust, anger and greed.

Great prophets may have healed physical blindness, but you are removing the cataracts of nescience from those deprived of their spiritual vision – a genuine miracle. Yet with disarming humility you disclaim all credit. As in one lecture in 1968, you administer the medicine, reveal the formula, but then exhibit you detachment:

Śrīla Prabhupāda: It is so simple, nice. Try to understand by your knowledge, question. We are not pushing forcibly... sometimes it is said that the Mohammedans, they propagated, one hand sword and one hand Koran. “Either you accept Koran, or there is sword for you.” It is not that. It is placed for your judgment. And if you like, you can accept it. Otherwise, I came here empty-handed, I shall go back empty-handed. There is no loss, no gain.

But you did not come with anything, you came with everything. You’re not a pharmacist from Calcutta, you’re a pharmacist from Vaikuṇṭha, delivering the salve of love of Kṛṣṇa. Not just any love, but the most exalted *mādhurya* mellow. The recipe for this balm is a long cherished family secret descending from Śrī Mādhavendra Purī, through Lord Caitanya Himself. You have encrypted that prescription within your writings and to decode it is a simple process (*Bg*. 4.32):

*tad viddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā*  
*upadekṣyanti te jñānaṁ jñāninas tattva-darśinaḥ*

Under the guidance of your empowered disciples, even we can be the recipients of this salve of love. If all the prerequisites are there and the process is followed, even bell-metal can be transformed into gold. In this way you are a spiritual alchemist, emissary of the golden *avatāra*.

In that lecture in 1968 you also demonstrated this quality as you appealed to you audience:

Śrīla Prabhupāda: It’s not difficult. Simply we must be willing. That’s all.. Is there any difficulty to accept this formula? I ask all of you.. You have asked so many questions of me. Is there any difficulty to accept?  
Young man: No.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda: So why don’t you take it?  
Young man: I have.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda: Thank you.

You are thanking, but we must thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for treating our illness and reviving our health. We admit ourselves to your care and submit our prayers to your lotus feet, that we are always prepared to accept the medicine, no matter how bitter it tastes due to our diseased senses. We pray that we may also participate in delivering the remedy to others, that we may win Lord Caitanya’s favor. We pray that we may never forget your unshakable faith in the order of your spiritual master, and that one day we might be able to say as you once did: “I didn’t look left of right, practically I walked through fire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness.”

*Your servants from Nuremberg Temple*,  
(by Bhakta-vatsala dāsa)

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

This event is very pleasing and auspicious because you appeared on this day. Your appearance gives the living entities a chance to have the association of a pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa, Your Divine Grace. The actual auspiciousness begins for a living entity when he gets an opportunity to come in touch with a pure devotee. It is due to your mercy and the miracle of your association that now devotees with the mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness are preaching all over the surface of the globe. Your unique contribution of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness will serve as a sublime example for *ācāryas* of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to come.

Every pastime you displayed, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is extraordinary. Your unflinching faith in the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja and the prediction of Lord Caitanya that His holy name would spread all over the surface of the earth is especially unique in the history of Vaiṣṇava ācāryas. Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura envisioned the mission of Lord Caitanya, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja gave inspiration and you successfully spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, especially in the Western countries. Even now it is due to your mercy and vision that Kṛṣṇa consciousness continues to spread all over, despite obstacles placed before preachers by demoniac forces.

Though we maintain the desire to please you, unfortunately, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have not done enough here in Pandharpur due to our lack of intelligence. We beg at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow your causeless mercy and give us enough intelligence so that we may become some insignificant part of your preaching movement, particularly in the Pandharpur region.

Pandharpur is the spiritual heart of Maharastra, where Lord Viṭṭhala is worshiped in a unique manner of *darśana* and *kīrtana*. Many *ācāryas* and saintly personalities have glorified this spiritual headquarters of the beloved saint Tukarāma. During His travels to South India, Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu came to Pandharpur. Viśvarūpa, the elder brother of Caitanya Mahāprabhu, attained *samādhi* here.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg again and again at your lotus feet, please be merciful to us to serve you eternally.

*Your insignificant servants, ISKCON Pandharpur devotees.*

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances to your dear lotus feet. All glories to you, savior of the fallen souls.

You appeared in this Kali-yuga to give to the *mlecchas* and *yavanas* all around the world the most precious gift of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your instructions are the life and soul of this ISKCON movement, which can only breathe properly by following them. If some members of our institution are now suffocating in any part of the world where you left the inheritance of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, this is because of not properly following your loving guidance.

”Books are the basis,” you said, and book distribution can definitely maintain our temples. Any other means of income will just prove to be nonpermanent. Depending on books, on the contrary, will bring stability. How can we construct a building without having a solid foundation? Not only the financial stability of our institution is depending on your transcendental books, but everything else. The faith of all the devotees, going through any type of circumstances, is completely nourished by reading your books. Even in the darkest moments, so many bewildered souls take complete shelter in them and are completely enlightened by their knowledge:

*kṛṣṇe sva-dhāmopagate*  
*dharma-jñānādibhiḥ saha*  
*kalau naṣṭa-dṛśām eṣa*  
*purāṇārko ‘dhunoditaḥ*

**”This** ***Bhāgavata Purāṇa*** **is as brilliant as the sun, and it has arisen just after the departure of Lord Kṛṣṇa to His own abode, accompanied by religion, knowledge, etc. Persons who have lost their vision due to the dense darkness of ignorance in the age of Kali shall get light from this** ***Purāṇa*****.” (*****Bhāg.*** **1.3.43)**

And, on top of everything, your presence is felt so strongly in your books which are like a gold mine. We can find in them everything we need: wise advice, good humor, sweet poetry, deep knowledge and mainly the way of attaining pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa.

We thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being constantly present in our Paris community. Your presence is the only remedy for this Kali-yuga.

As you know, we have the desire to move your divine self and Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvāra, Śrī Jagannāth, Baladeva, Subhadrā, and Śrī Nitāi-Śacisūta to new premises for better preaching facilities. Paris is such a big city, offering so many opportunities for people to hear your message. We want to take better advantage of that, but our desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness is so pale compared to yours; this is why things are going much slower than when you were personally present amongst us. Because you have a pure desire, Kṛṣṇa fulfills it, and this is why we want to ask you to personally intervene at this specific point of our search. Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Parisīśvara will certainly give you Their blessings and, as your followers, we just need to be ready whenever They will decide to give Their mercy through you.

Since preaching in Paris means preaching at once all over France and even many other countries around the world, please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, help us make that a reality so that we will be able to assist you in your wonderful preaching mission here in Paris.

*Your dependent servants in Paris.*

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Once in 1969, during a question period at the end of one of your classes, a simple-hearted Indian boy inquired from you as to whether you were “happy!” You answered as follows “In India, I left my children, but here, although I came alone, Kṛṣṇa has given me hundreds of nice children...” Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are all extremely happy to be here as children in your house in which the whole world can live. And yet at the same time, we all know that you are expecting much more from us as your followers than some sentiment of childlike dependence.

In a letter you once wrote to Brahmānanda Prabhu, you spoke of how you had acquired so many “young American fathers!” You continued to elaborate on how, when you were a child, you used to annoy your father by “demanding so many unreasonable things.” You proceeded to explain that the very same nature continues to be displayed as you place demands on your “loving American fathers!” Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, while we may be feeling very safe and secure as dependent children in you ISKCON house, we must also be prepared to take up the gauntlet. Just as your good father bent over backwards in the face of all of your “unreasonable childhood demands,” we must display the same “fatherly” love and dedication as we attempt to meet all of your very challenging orders and instructions!

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to you that we may never, ever become “problem children.” And, needless to say, an irresponsible father who neglects his duties is similarly a great burden on the family. We pray that we may continue to live together, serve each other and work hard for you forever.

*Your servants of ISKCON Perth Yātrā, Australia*.

## Potomac

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet on this auspicious day of your appearance ninety-eight years ago. As time passes year after year we are more able to realize your greatness by what you achieved in such a short span of time. As we engage in presenting Kṛṣṇa consciousness in this area, we get daily reminders of the insurmountable potency of the material energy on the conditioned souls. How were you, like a mighty warrior, able to cut through the forces of *māyā* with the powerful sword of transcendental knowledge?

We miss you greatly, without your personal presence here in America we feel the intense effects of materialism all around us closing in. However you have showed us the way to surpass the influence of māyā through the process of *śaranāgati*, surrender to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa, and we daily pray for your guidance and inspiration.

On this special day we take the time to offer this pray for your continued mercy. We are slow, fallen sense-gratifiers who cheat in our devotional practices. And we always find excuses to put off serving till the next day. We are expert at making plans for our future stay in this material world and we lack the desire to preach the message of Lord Kṛṣṇa. We are lazy and unclean and lack determination, therefore it takes us many years to accomplish the mission you have given us.

Our only hope is to grab on tightly to the lotus feet of our savior in this horrible age of Kali, Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda, who are the deliverers of the most unqualified, and pray that somehow we get some special mercy to continue to assist you in your service to Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants at ISKCON Potomac, Maryland*.

## Rāḍhadeśa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. The devotees of Rāḍhadeśa Dhāma are feeling so indebted to you and are pleased to have the opportunity to glorify you along with your faithful followers throughout the world. As the years roll by and we are allowed to continue engaging in the service of your mission, the love and appreciation of each member of community, old and new, simply increases.

A new *bhakta* is unloading rice into a storage bin and accidentally spills some grains. I tell the story how you once tearfully told a disciple, “Don’t waste one grain – it is Kṛṣṇa’s gift.” Without a word he instantly stoops to retrieve the grains.

My assistant in the kitchen is always anxious to hear your lectures and the narration of your glorious literatures by Amala-bhakta dāsa. As she swiftly and silently does her service, it is not unusual to see a stream of tears from her eyes. Soon she will join the *saṅkīrtana* party and start distributing your books.

My dear friend, a disciple of His Holiness Lokanātha Mahārāja, came to live in Rāḍhadeśa on his instruction. Her husband had left his body hearing and chanting the holy name, and she says that she wants to also leave her body in that glorious manner, holding a *kalaśa* with which she will pour transcendental water over your beloved form in 1996. Every morning we behold your divine *mūrtī* form, cleansed and nicely dressed by your faithful grandson, a disciple of His Holiness Hridayānanda Mahārāja.

His Holiness Suhotra Mahārāja prays to you on your disappearance day that he may be eternally engaged as the servant of your servant a thousand times removed. When we revealed some of the difficulties we had in moving forward in our spiritual life to His Holiness Prahlādānanda Mahārāja, he gently reminded us, “Do it for Śrīla Prabhupāda.” His Holiness Sastsvarūpa Mahārāja says at the time of death we will have to inform the Lord’s representatives that “I am a follower of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda,” and that will gain us their recognition and consequent entry into the Lord’s association.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I sometimes lament that I never was really able to enter into your association on the few occasions that I was in your blessed presence, but it is a fact that by the association and service of your followers we are experiencing your presence more and more. And your presence is nod-different from Lord Kṛṣṇa’s. He instructs us to surrender to Him and you also give that instruction. Actually that is impossible for a conditioned soul to do, but your inconceivable mercy makes the impossible something we aspire to achieve. You, as His representative, are so attractive that we can remain engaged in practical service to your mission and gradually purify our diseased hearts by always hearing the Lord’s glories as they have been realized and explained by Your Divine Grace.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to bless us with your protection. We pray to be more and more absorbed in your service so that we can please you by one day becoming really pure devotees capable of fulfilling your desire to deliver ourselves and all sincere souls from the clutches of *māyā* into the shelter of devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants at Rāḍhadeśa Dhāma in Belgium.*

## Rome

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet on this most auspicious day of your transcendental appearance.

You have graced the entire world with your infinite mercy and every living entity is benefiting from your immeasurable compassion. As a result of your extraordinary mission, even we mlecchas have received access to the highest comprehension of the Absolute Truth, which had never before been revealed to the Western civilization.

In Rome itself, thousands of Christian saints have passed, such as Saint Peter, Saint Paul, Saint Francis and Saint Catherine, all with the mission of preaching God consciousness, yet never were there chanted such confidential and powerful names of God as you revealed to us when you touched your lotus feet on the Western shores: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare; Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

As a perfect master, you have completed the divine mission of all preceding saints, revealing the ultimately personal aspect of the Supreme: *kṛṣṇas tu bhagavān svayaṁ,* Śrī Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Refuting the dogmas and speculations of the impersonalistic doctrines, as a perfect disciple *– namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe virviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe* – you have fulfilled the desire of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura by establishing the doctrine of personalism and liberating the Western countries which are pervaded by voidist and impersonalistic philosophies.

You have crowned with success the efforts of the entire *guru-paramparā* and all the sincere souls who sacrificed their lives over the centuries to penetrate into the “mystery of faith.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, in honor of the 20thanniversary of your visit here in the opening of your first Italian temple, ISKCON Rome now offers you a substantial preaching structure composed of a self-sufficient farm on the outskirts of Rome and a preaching center in the heart of the historical city. Just as you had instructed in one of your morning walks here in Rome.

You have the perfect vision of the past, present and future, and you continue guiding us to success giving us, in turn, the credit. And we, blinded by false ego, perceive your infinite mercy in our efforts only when the results manifest fully the divine characteristics that are beyond our capacity, revealing themselves just as you had desired.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us become perfect instruments in your lotus hands. That we may serve you in realizing your divine preaching mission in the transcendental pastimes of *saṅkīrtana-jajña.* We thank you infinitely.

*Your aspiring servants, ISKCON Rome*.

## Scotland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our respectful obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on the most auspicious day of your appearance in this world. All glories to your ISKCON. All glories to the saṅkīrtana movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

”My spiritual master, he told me, “if you ever get money then print books.” This statement by your Guru Mahārāja, Śrīla Prabhupāda, sparked off a worldwide revolution that, as predicted by the Supreme Lord Himself would eventually reach every town and village of the world. In your lifetime you stressed this activity so much. You even went so far as to say that from this book distribution, everything will come. Now we can see the effect that this transcendental book distribution is having, even in the most unlikely parts of the world. In the pages of your books the voice of the Supreme Authority Lord Kṛṣṇa is to be heard, and His purpose is so nicely explained in your purports. We are factually only able to gather for this Vyāsa-pūjā and offer some glorification because of what we read in the pages of your books. These books are proper guidelines turning the lives and thoughts of all who read them in an honest, openhearted manner. You made this translation work your life’s mission, knowing full well the necessity for this knowledge to be made available in all possible languages and to all cultures.

You know the Absolute Truth will have its effect upon whoever comes in contact with it, either knowingly or unknowingly, and therefore you worked so hard to give as much as possible in a few short years. *Bhagavad-gītā*, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, and *The Science of Self-Realization*, introduce the misguided living entities to their real self – opening the doorway to their eternal identity as the servants of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa. The fruits of your transcendental work are there for all to see, but for those with mundane vision, the far reaching effects of your endeavors will not be understood. For the devotee, if he takes time to meditate upon what you gave, and being blessed by the mercy of *guru* and Kṛṣṇa, a mood of indebtedness will arise within his heart and mind.

In the true meaning of the words, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave everything that needed to be given. As the representative of the *paramparā*, you gave the Supreme Lord, His name, His qualities, His pastimes and His devotees in an unadulterated, pure fashion – your mercy upon a struggling mankind. All over the world you established so many shelters where those who had become tired of the society of cheaters and the cheated could take refuge. To try to thank you for what you gave is an endless task, but if we sincerely take up your mission, by this we know you will be pleased. There is no end to the service and preaching that needs to be done, so our future is bright and busy.

On this day, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we would like to take the opportunity to pray for the benediction to be blessed with the realization of just how important your books are and to see the necessity for their distribution. By your kindness we have been given a wealth of spiritual knowledge. Our only duty now is to take such instruction to heart, act upon it and preach it to others.

All glories to your ISKCON. All glories to the distribution of your books and all glories to the merciful saṅkīrtana movement.

*Your insignificant servants from the Scottish Yātrā.*

## Singapore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our most beloved spiritual master.

On this most auspicious day, we are humbly offering you our deep, heartfelt appreciation of your transcendental mercy on all of us here. We still cannot deeply appreciate how you are saving us from this ocean of *māyā*. Here in Singapore, we have tried to establish the transcendental message of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and, by slowly taking shelter of your divine instructions, we are seeing that it is all coming true. This is making us wonder why we have not taken complete shelter of your divine instructions much sooner.

We are in the process of acquiring some land to build our first Kṛṣṇa conscious temple. We have already opened our first restaurant to distribute Śrī Kṛṣṇa *prasāda* at the most prestigious shopping area of Singapore. We are also in the process of opening a Kṛṣṇa conscious farm for the protection of cows and preservation of brahminical culture. We have increased our book distribution, our membership, our congregation, etc., and we can see that this is all due to our trying to take shelter of Your Divine Grace.

This is all nothing as you have a much bigger mercy plan for all of us, that is, you want us to receive Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa *premā.* Our only regret is that we do not have more faith in Your Divine Grace. As a result, we can only weep as you hand it out, and we do not receive it. How many more lifetimes will we take to get it? Please forgive us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we are the most neophyte of all your divine sons and daughters. Kindly excuse us for our long delay in trying to fulfill you many transcendental desires in this area owing to our many faults.

*Your most unworthy servants in Singapore*.

## Slovenia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. You are the only one who can bring us and all other living entities back home to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Our desire is to be always engaged in your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness with all our being.

On your Vyāsa-pūjā we can remember your transcendental personality with great reverence and gratitude. You illuminated for us the path towards real success in life. You have told us who actually we are – spirit souls, eternal servants of Lord Kṛṣṇa, how simple and wonderful. We will never be able to really understand your causeless mercy, which over flooded this world, the world of selfishness, hypocrisy and forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is said that one can recognize a great personality by his activities. And whose contribution to this world could compare with even a fraction of your magnanimous contribution? You gave Kṛṣṇa consciousness, a matchless gift, to the living entities. You suffered innumerable troubles, just to fulfill the order of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura. The order was that you go to the West and to spread the mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu all over the world.

You accepted the order of your spiritual master as your heart and soul, and that was your success. Now you already have more than 400 centers of Kṛṣṇa consciousness all around the world. Thousands and thousands of devotees are surrendered at your command, chanting the holy names with great determination and love, and giving Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others. You yourself said that the secret of success lies in surrendering to the mission of one’s spiritual master.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your transcendental books are your main weapon, and by your causeless mercy, they are being distributed everywhere in the world, thus gradually destroying the demoniac mentality of people in this age of Kali. The holy name of the Lord is already chanted in every country, and we hope to live long enough to see the day when the majority of men will accept Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and in this way fulfill the prophecy of Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Last year you very mercifully came to our temple in Ljubljana in the form of your *mūrtī.* In this way you inspired us neophytes with much new strength and enthusiasm in our fight against māyā. We beg you for the benediction to be always able to act in this transcendental mission as your instruments, so that you can use us according to your desire.

We are all very grateful to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! By your causeless mercy you have given us a chance to serve you, but we still need much of your special mercy to be successful in that service. We need your mercy to be able to distribute more books, to preach more powerfully, to make new devotees and to spread your glories all over the world. Please, give us a chance to please you by connecting all of the fallen souls with Kṛṣṇa again. By you causeless mercy, let Kṛṣṇa consciousness flood all of Slovenia, and the whole world.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
*Your humble servants from Ljubljana, Slovenia*.

## South Africa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On behalf of your disciples, grand disciples and followers of the Durban Yātrā, we wish to submit ourselves at your lotus feet and beg you to please accept our feeble attempts at trying to please you.

You very kindly visited this remote tip of the Dark Continent in October of 1975, almost 20 years ago. At that time, the South African Yātrā consisted of only one rented house, two vehicles, and ten devotees. Since then, by your inspiration, we have established our headquarters for South Africa in Durban – the Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānātha Mandira.

Presently, we are engaged in a variety of preaching programs. Our Food for Life department distributes 20,000 plates of prasādam a week. Our traveling festival program is conducting 70 festivals this year. 100,000 people are attracted to the annual Ratha-yātrā Festival, which is held over a five-day Christmas period. The Bhaktivedanta College of Education and Culture is offering certificates, diplomas and degrees in the study of your books, and we have over 200 students. Preaching at the local universities, conducting seminar courses and tours are being regularly maintained. We now have over 2220 BTG subscribers. We also have a very enthusiastic and responsible congregation together with 30 nāma-hattas. Large festivals for Rāma-navamī, Gaura-Pūrṇimā, and Kṛṣṇa-Janmāṣṭhamī are attended by over 200,000 people.

All of this is for your pleasure; without your mercy we cannot advance in our practice of *sādhana-bhakti*. By maintaining and helping to expand your preaching mission in South Africa, we can clean our hearts of all the *anarthas* (impurities) and *aparādhas* (offenses). As we traverse the *anartha-nivṛtti* stage, we can come to the stage of steady devotional service, and by further mercy, eventually come *to ruci, āsakti, bhāva* and ultimately *premā*, or pure love of God.

Higher states of *bhakti* can only be attained by the mercy of an intimate associate of the Lord. No doubt you are such a person. As such, you are able to bestow upon your followers the highest good fortune, which is a genuine attraction and taste for *śravanam*, *kīrtanam* and *smaranam* of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvana.

We sincerely hope that our humble attempts to please you are accepted and that you can favorably glance at all your followers in South Africa.

Our ardent desire and prayer is to expand the influence of your preaching mission and further establish ISKCON as the only shelter for the unfortunate conditioned souls of South Africa.

Please accept our feeble attempts at trying to please you and continue to guide and inspire us to serve you with real affection.

Just as Raghunātha dāsa Gosvāmī’s attachment to Rūpa Gosvāmī is of the highest and most intimate level, we also pray that one day we can have the same attachment and affection for Your Divine Grace.

Begging to remain engaged in your service,  
*South African Yātrā.*

## St. Louis

Service to your mission will please you that we know.  
Regular chanting of your glories helps the *bhakti-latā* grow.  
Indifference to sense enjoyment, giving up our own desires,  
Loving service to your lotus feet puts out the burning fire.  
Always we must remember you until our final hour.

Praying very fervently to get your strong direction,  
Running to get your shelter from *māyā’s* poisonous infection,  
Anxious for the fallen souls with no knowledge of life’s aim,  
Bringing them to know your books so they can make real gains,  
Handing out to others Kṛṣṇa-*prasādam* to cure their tongue,  
Unswerving in our efforts to see your holy work is done,  
Putting down the madness of a mindless, Godless way,  
Allowing only the truth you’ve taught to have the final say,  
Doing all these things because we truly want to please you,  
Always we hope in our heart of hearts that soon again we’ll see you.

*Your children in St. Louis, Missouri*.

## Stockholm

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Please accept my most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace!

How simply you presented the most sublime knowledge. Even great thinkers, yogīs and mystics couldn’t find the ultimate meaning of the Vedas. Then you came alone to the kingdom of the mlecchas and cleared the path for them to go back home, back to Godhead. Simple and sublime was your message and simple and sublime the process to receive it.

”Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy,” you said. It’s so simple. Then why is to so difficult to taste and realize it?

Despite having such a profound knowledge and being able to speak on any subject, you strictly presented the essence in the most practical and understandable form. As such, even the most dull-headed person can understand that Kṛṣṇa is the Absolute Truth, we are spirit souls, not this material body, and now we must surrender unto Him.

”It’s simple for the simple and complicated for the complicated” – a tremendous knowledge presented with the innocence of a child. Appearing like this, you baffled the minds of so-called intellectuals, who, following their unchaste, lusty minds, are always trying to look beyond Kṛṣṇa, pushing forward their own biased views, which consequently cheat the ignorant public. Your words are brilliant, clear and deep. They can at any time transform people’s minds, helping them realize the original Kṛṣṇa consciousness within their hearts. Who did such a thing before in the Western world? No one.

You gave us *Bhagavad-gītā* As It Is and Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead as He is. You never allowed us to degrade our consciousness from spiritual to mental, from devotional service to psychoanalysis, from ecstatic saṅkīrtana to introverted research. You showed us that the beauty of devotional service is in its simplicity and depth. You brought us from the flickering mental platform to the happy, simple life of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let me always remember the value of simplicity and not allow my mind to push me into the illusory realm of understanding what is beyond Your instructions or beyond Your books. Please Śrīla Prabhupāda, let me taste the same prasāda again and again, the same saṅkīrtana again, the same darśana again, the same guru-pūjā again – pure and uncontaminated – as you gave it. There is so much more to know within the instructions you gave and there is so much to give from the treasure that is contained in your simple and precise words. Why should we attempt to add to it? Why look for more somewhere else? There is no more to get! You gave everything – the beginning, the middle and the final goal. As gold is gold in any stage, the golden Kṛṣṇa consciousness you gave is also perfect in all stages. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, save me from the hungry mind which always wants to go “beyond.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you never grow old. As Kṛṣṇa is nava-yauvana, eternally fresh, so You are also eternally fresh, living in your books. Whoever stays under your shelter also stays young despite the aging body and melancholic mind. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let me always feel close to saṅkīrtana bag, ready to go distribute your books again. I always want to be your *saṅkīrtana* fanatic, joining your saṅkīrtana parties wherever you are. I always want to join your bhakti, taste your prasāda and hear Your lectures – wherever you are.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, save me from being old, introverted, melancholic ? and captured by nostalgia. Let me simply appreciate what you gave, and give it as it is to others. Then maybe they also can understand that we are not this bodies, nor are we meant to suffer.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving me – now I have to try to surrender.  
Your servant in Hare Kṛṣṇa Center – Stockholm.

(Written by Manidhāra dāsa)

## Sydney

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Many are the ways and the means, the truths and the moods of the spiritual path. For the serious disciple, however, they are all rolled into one thing and one thing alone – the divine lotus feet of the spiritual master. Śrīla Prabhupāda, as we slowly awaken to the responsibility of your legacy and rise to confront māyā’s multifaceted illusions, we need pause again and again to reflect on this perennial truth, for within it lies the key to our own identity, who and what we really are, both as an individual and as ISKCON. Through such consideration we derive the indicators for valid success.

Ignorant materialism and its superficial accomplishments are at best an imposing bluff, and pseudo-spirituality is only a fog quickly dispersed by the Kṛṣṇa sun. However, the realms of a bona fide transcendence is possessed of truly disturbing and daunting phenomenon, and therein lie the really alarming challenges. The vistas conjured up by an evolving awareness of the transcendental possibilities and options pose a serious threat to the confident retention of perspective, especially as our hitherto self-assurance becomes exposed as perhaps nothing more than naive complacency.

How to proceed? Feign a confidence we do not feel and flatter our way into the ecumenical brotherhood until we can justify our pride through their acceptance? Presume ourselves replete with the maturity and requisite discretion for availing ourselves the opportunities for ‘higher’ education? Or, like a minnow fleeing sharks, should we scurry for cover in the face of intellectual and devotional challenge? What, Śrīla Prabhupāda, as your disciples and grand disciples, should be our response to all the temptations and offers of perfection lying about in the transcendental realm? Where does loyalty end, and narrow-mindedness in fact begin? Is there actually a difference even, and, if so, does it really matter?

In the midst of the interminable back and forth such questions raise, however, we do well to remind ourselves that for the disciple, reality is no longer entirely optional. In fact, for the disciple, the lotus feet of his spiritual master constitute for him the entirety of reality, or whatever for him there may be of it, both now and forever. Those two divine feet are the reservoir of eternal shelter for that disciple, and it is within their bounds that he will find his own eternal definition. Outside of an absolute commitment to those lotus feet, his existence itself effectively fails and reality is void for him. To lease aside those lotus feet under the delusion of ‘option’ is an existential tragedy for the *jīva* soul. By the grace of Kṛṣṇa one is delivered unto them. The unseemly arrogance that gives rise to their neglect is unwelcome. Instead, dedication to them matures in the disciple as loyalty to the *guru*. That loyalty in turn dictates his conviction that beyond the lotus feet of his spiritual master there *is* in fact no reality. Or rather, even if there is, and even if it may seem exceedingly wonderful, for him, at least it commands no interest and may as well not exist. It is within such “myopia” that the disciple may discover an abiding contentment. Such contentment is free of all existential hankering and within that contentment lies the secret of eternal peace, for how is it possible to feel any unease if one hankers not? Perhaps it is some kind of illusion to be so absorbed, but so long as it continues forever, who cares? As disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda, we certainly don’t!

In your case, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have gone far beyond mere contentment; we are in ecstasy! We have transcended mere loyalty, we are bursting with pride! We are not concerned with so-called reality, we have seen the Truth! If meeting a bona fide spiritual master is the height of fortune, by Kṛṣṇa’s grace, we have scaled the pinnacle. Even if the future is a mere extrapolation of the present, within creation we have nothing left for which to aspire. So, to whatever stroke of fate or fortune we may owe the fact of contact with your divine lotus feet, we are not about to look that gift horse in the mouth. Far be it for us the crassness of ingratitude, the ignorance of discontent. Let submission be the indication of intelligence and exclusivity the essence of virtue. Whatever may be beyond that, let it remain there.

*Your servants at ISKCON, Sydney, Australia*.

## Tallahassee

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my humble *daṇḍavat praṇāmas* in remembrance of your appearance on this most auspicious day. You have taught us that the conditioned soul has imperfect senses, is sure to commit mistakes, and is invariably illusioned. In other words, to err is human. Yet, within ourselves there is an element that is attracted by perfection. If it were not so, we might remain content with our present acquisition.

The conditioned soul would not have such an attraction for perfection if he were not in need of help. The Supreme Absolute Truth is neither supreme nor absolute if He cannot, of His own free will, resurrect the fallen conditioned soul from the suffocating fire of material existence. For this purpose the Lord comes Himself in different incarnations, or He sends His confidential servant, Śrī Gurudeva.

Ninety-eight years ago, on this most auspicious day you made your appearance by the request of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura to fulfill the merciful will of the previous *ācāryas*.

Although we possess no *saṁskāra* or acquired pious activities, you have mercifully asserted yourself on our behalf and thus created our pious activities. Only then could we become fit recipients of Kṛṣṇa’s mercy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I am helpless and hopeless and mired in disappointment, but you are the fittest servitor of the Lord and you are empowered by the Lord to reinstate such misguided souls as myself to their constitutional position. I pray with all the sincerity at my disposal, on this most auspicious and happy day, that by your causeless mercy, I may remember that the Supreme Absolute Truth, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is my eternal master and that I, the jīva soul, am His eternal constitutional subordinate. Therefore, I once again offer my most humble *daṇḍavat praṇāmas* at your lotus feet.

*Your servants in Tallahassee, Florida*.

## Trinidad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

To advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness we must at last abandon the envy that brought us here and which still holds us back from fully loving Kṛṣṇa, loving you and loving each other. We can give up that envy if we strictly follow your instructions, for they contain everything required for destroying all bad qualities and developing pure love for Kṛṣṇa. Although in the darkness of our own ignorance we often lose sight of your instructions, you never stop guiding us. You are always at the helm, expertly directing us, both as a society and as individuals, through the dangerous Kali-yuga waters.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength to see the journey through to its end. Help us tolerate the rough seas, knowing that, although still in the ocean, we are, by your mercy, above it. And help us cooperate with our fellow passengers in fulfilling your mission to rescue other drowning souls so they may also cross the ocean to the safe shore of the shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet.

Although the ocean is vast and deep it cannot compare to the vastness of your mercy and compassion for the fallen souls nor to the depth of your love for Kṛṣṇa. May we always glorify your wonderful spiritual qualities with our every breath and express our gratitude to you by dedicating our lives to your mission of saving the lost souls from the ocean of birth and death and taking them back home to Godhead.

*Your servants at ISKCON Longdenville, Trinidad*.

## Udhampur

Our most dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Hare Kṛṣṇa,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus-like divine feet. All glories to your divine personality, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are the savior of the whole world and of the most fallen souls like ourselves by giving us the transcendental service of the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa here at Śrīla Prabhupāda āśrama under the able guidance of your most dear disciple H.H. Nava Yogendra Swami

Śrīla Prabhupāda you are the ocean of mercy and kindness. You are always worried about our welfare because we are always forgetful conditioned souls. You know that we cannot see Śrī Kṛṣṇa face to face, which you see all the time, because of our defective senses. Śrīla Prabhupāda you gave us the easiest approach to realize the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa – rendering devotional service. We are realizing day by day how powerful this process is in this tough age of Kali. People try to do meditation by sitting in different yogic postures, but devotional service is the best way to meditate. You are the most perfect master and guide and know how to keep neophytes like us busy in Kṛṣṇa’s service, thereby keeping us away from *māyā*. Otherwise, *māyā* is so strong that she is always on alert to defeat us. By your causeless mercy only are we able to defeat it. That mercy is always there, provided we follow your divine instructions without any deviation.

We beg again and again at your lotus feet: please don’t let us drift away in the waves of this material ocean of miseries. Please Śrīla Prabhupāda:

SAVE US! SAVE US! SAVE US! SAVE US!

*We remain your insignificant and menial servants birth after birth here at Śrīla Prabhupāda āśrama – Udhampur, India.*

## Uppsala

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**The Only Hope**

Endless attachments to the material world,  
always seeking to enjoy her forms,  
so unhappy and overcome by grief,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Clinging to memories of the past,  
”they taste so nice and give relief,”  
total illusion for spiritual life,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Strong attraction for respect and fame,  
”yes, I am a great devotee,”  
humbleness not to be found,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Aversion to everything in Kṛṣṇa’s service,  
spontaneously embracing what *māyā* gives,  
will this fight never end?  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Faultfinding, enviousness and deceitfulness,  
constant companions of this poor life,  
forever regretting making friends with them,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Performing the *sevā* just out of habit,  
where is the taste? Where is the *premā*?  
Still – after so many years!  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

Entering the *līlā* in Goloka Vṛndāvana,  
with selfless devotion to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa,  
when will that happen?  
Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mercy – the only hope.

*Uppsala, Sweden*.  
(Written by Prapūjaka dāsa)

## Vancouver

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to you as the *paramahaṁsa, parivrājakācārya* because you are the supermost swan-like devotee who has traversed the globe, bestowing your grace upon the fallen souls. The most amazing thing is that, despite your being swan-like and our being crow-like, we have been able to understand your message at all. How is it possible for crows to understand or even appreciate anything of swans? It is said that birds of a feather flock together, not caring for the others who aren’t like them. If that is so, then how is it that you had such compassion on all of the crow-like persons who are simply searching in the refuse of this material world for putrid things to enjoy? How is it that you, the most swan-like personality, had any care for the crows of this world? But what to speak of caring and compassion, you had genuine love for all the crow-like persons who are loitering here. Because of that genuine love you left the association of the other swan-like devotees in Vṛndāvana and came to associate with the crows in the hope of giving them your gift. If crows cannot understand of appreciate swans, then how is it that you were able to influence the crows of the world to not only appreciate some value in your gift, but to also accept your gift?

This is not an easy to do. But we know how it was done, because you have taught us how everything is accomplished in Lord Kṛṣṇa’s creation – by desire. It was your desire to save all of the crow-like persons rotting in *māyā’s* garbage heap. Somehow you, a very special swan-like personality, desired the upliftment of all the crow-like souls. That is your great gift to the world. And it wasn’t just for some few lucky souls. Somehow you had genuine concern for all the souls of this world.

By your desire some of us crows are being transformed into more beautiful swan-like birds. Soft, white swan-like feathers are gradually replacing our hard, black crow-like feathers as an external manifestation of the internal changes of our consciousness. Crows becoming swans? How can such a thing be possible? It must be a ruse, for if it is not, it is a most amazing miracle indeed. Yet a miracle it is. To be sure there is swan-like plumage where there never was before.

Oh, how fortunate we are! Look at us – we are becoming swan-like, and aren’t we grand! Aren’t we now better than those other crows that are still all black? The very fact that we have more white fathers than the next bird, does that not mean that we are better than him? Of course it must, otherwise why would we have the white feathers instead of them?

Although the body is beginning to change the crow-like consciousness definitely has some way to go.

Not only do we criticize our former friends of a feather, but having simply begun this most amazing transformation, we have also taken to swimming the waters of the fully transformed, the waters of the genuine swans. Having come there at your expense, we now think that we even deserve this special place. Have we forgotten that we have an obligation for being here, either to you or to anyone else? Do we stop to consider that obligation? How unfortunate we are – the crow-like consciousness still shows itself despite the presence of some soft, white swan-like feathers.

We are becoming swan-like only by your desire, and only by your grace. It is obvious that we are not fully swans because we lack the most obvious characteristic of swans (obvious to the swans, but totally incomprehensible to the crows) – that of compassion. When the transformation is more complete, the compassion will begin to dawn in our hearts. When the transformation is still more complete, that compassion will galvanize the action to leave the lake of swans and go back to the garbage heap and find more crows to bring your message to, so that by your grace they can also transform into swan-like men.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this observance of your appearance in this world of crows we fall down at your lotus feet and offer our prostrated obeisances to you. You are the topmost of all swan-like beings. We have no qualification for having achieved your shelter; it is by your grace, and your grace only that we have begun this transformation to become swan-like beings. We pray with all humility that we be allowed to remember always our crow-like existence so that we will not become proud of a few soft, swan-like feathers, and foolishly continue to act alike the crows we once were. You are the topmost swan-like being, and the most merciful and compassionate as well. Let us recognize the debt that we have to you, and attempt to repay your kindness by taking the trouble in our lives of extending your grace to others, until your desire is fully realized, and all of the crow-like beings of this world bear a new, more brilliant plumage, and become qualified to swim in the waters of the fully transformed.

*Your servants of Vancouver ISKCON*.  
(by Dhaneśvara dāsa)

## Villagio Hare Kṛṣṇa

My dear spiritual master, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please accept my humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

By reading your transcendental books, especially the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, I come closer to an understanding of the art of associating and serving the devotees. In the beginning you were so compassionate with all of us. We didn’t know even the most common sense behavior toward life. Yet with great patience and compassion you engage us in Kṛṣṇa’s service. Actually spiritual life is full of rules, dos and don’ts but you make everything so appealing and simple that for me it appears encouraging and possible to undertake. I look back at the way you so lovingly took great care of me through your dedicated representative. It is astonishing. You made so many miracles all over the world. Just consider the great, gigantic work of translating *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. What a valuable gift for the Western World! As the *Bhagavad-Gītā* mentions:

**”The working senses are superior to dull matter; mind is higher than the senses; intelligence is still higher than; and he \[the soul\] is even higher than the intelligence.” (*****Bg*****. 3.42)**

And you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are superior to everyone. You are the most dear associate and representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa. You are teaching the great science of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to many millions of people. Lord Caitanya gave the instruction to preach everywhere and to everyone: you fulfill the desire of Lord Viśvambhara.

Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu was the best preacher of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in India. You are the best of preacher of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in the entire world because you are in the loving mood serving Lord Caitanya cent percent. For many thousands of years your books will be *law books,* manuals for spiritual education for the entire human race. The most wonderful thing is to read your book, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, to associate with you personally. It is wonderful to apply your teachings, the results are wonderful, and it is wonderful to listen to your devotees speak on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. This gives the highest taste. The glorification of your devotional work in presenting *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* can never be an exaggeration of praise. Every time I read, think, listen, and practice the teaching of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, I become more and more indebted to you. You have so much love for Kṛṣṇa that you revealed Him as He is without changing anything. This can only be possible for a pure devotee like you. It is mentioned in the First Canto that there is no difference between the book *bhāgavata* and the person *bhāgavata*. So by becoming attached to you I also appreciate the message of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* This is an open secret. It is said that by serving a great soul, a pure devotee, great service is done and one becomes attached to the message of Vāsudeva. My deep desire is that I can always read *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* in the association of your devotees and learn to glorify you, my spiritual master, deeper and deeper.

You are so kind, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to have come to the Western World, including Europe to preach the message of *bhāgavata-dharma*. You are saving and delivering so many fallen souls. Kṛṣṇa says that no one is dearer to Him than a preacher. Thank you. I should pray and act so that many spirit souls can come to ISKCON, serve you in helping to preach *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and apply your transcendental instructions.

All glories to you lotus feet.

*Your insignificant servants in ISKCON Villagio Hare Kṛṣṇa*.  
(by Durgama dāsa)

## Vṛndāvana

”Come, please come and live in Vṛndāvana.”  
”Can’t live in the Third World.” Too far from heaven.  
Before meeting your grace, this isn’t hard to believe,  
But after hearing from you, impossible to conceive.

For next to Vṛndāvana all other worlds pall.  
This holy *dhāma* isn’t the Third World at all.  
Right here on Earth, fully unfurled,  
A glorious extension of the spiritual world.

But without you we never would have known,  
Taste for Vṛndāvana would never have grown.  
Everywhere the vibration of Kṛṣṇa’s sweet holy name,  
Day and night peacocks punctuate the refrain.

Thousands of temples for serving the Lord,  
No one challenges, “Can you show me God?”  
So little by little we come to realize,  
That you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have opened our eyes.

Bu we are so dull; we forget your good graces.  
We fall back into *māyā’s* embraces.  
So although we live in the holiest *dhāma*,  
Our mundane lapses disturb the calm.

And now we are here, what is our duty?  
Shall we enjoy this spiritual booty?

Yes, in a sense, that’s there to be sure,  
But let’s not forget there is much more.

For although you gave us this safe, spiritual heaven,  
You also said, “Don’t look for Kṛṣṇa in some bush in Vṛndāvana.”  
You want us to understand and preach far and wide.  
It’s not your intention that we enjoy here and hide.

You came to this dhāma, for years prepared here.  
Then took Śrī Vṛndāvana with you everywhere.  
From your example, this is perfectly clear:  
Following your footsteps will make us most dear.

All we must do is repeat your message,  
Given fallen souls a chance at safe passage  
Our of the mire, out of the muck.  
Your grace alone can turn their luck.

And if we can do this just to please you,  
You’ll give us the eyes to see Vṛndāvana anew.  
And to please you in this way is not really so hard.  
From our hearts we thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Temple – Vṛndāvana, India*.  
(by Kuṇḍalī dāsa)

## Zagreb

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble and respectful obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

On this all-auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we pray to you to bless and empower us to appropriately glorify you.

You have extinguished the fire of our conditional life of repeated birth and death. You are the personification of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya. You are the greatest benediction for all the conditioned souls in this material world, since with your books, you are spreading the rays of the benediction moon. You have inspired the life of all transcendental knowledge in all of us. With you mission you have spread the ocean of transcendental bliss and enabled us to fully relish the nectar for which we are always anxious, pure love of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa.

O dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, your lotus feet can render all benediction to living beings. Just as the thin line of night at down surrenders to the sun, let our lives, which were like darkness, surrender and always stay under protection of your lotus feet that are shining like millions of suns.

Please bless us to become humble, tolerant, devoid of all sense of false prestige, and ready to offer all respect to others, so that in such a state of mind, we can chant the holy name of the Lord constantly.

Please bless us not to have any desire to accumulate wealth nor to hanker for opposite sex or a great number of followers. Allow us only to serve you purely and with devotion life after life.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are eternal servitors of your servants but somehow or other we have fallen into this ocean of birth and death. Please deliver us from this ocean of death and place us as one of the atoms at the lotus feet of your servants.

Let our eyes be decorated with tears of pure love and attachment while we are loudly singing in *kīrtana*: “*Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda, *Jaya* Śrīla Prabhupāda.”

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, feeling separation from you, we understand that the only way to be always with you is to assemble around your mission like spokes around the axle of a wheel and to continue following your instructions without any deviation.

Thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*The servants of your servants, ISKCON, Zagreb, Croatia*.

## Vyāsa-Pūjā Homages from Publishers

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North America

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you on this most auspicious day.

From Vṛndāvana you came, and returned to the same.  
Our young hearts were shattered that fateful day.  
Under illusion we thought on this earth you would always stay.  
Now we are forced to associate with you in a different way.  
Degraded lives from which we came,  
Eradicated by you, through chanting the holy name,  
Reading your books, regularly every day,

And serving you mission throughout our lives, we pray.  
Caring, as you, for the plight of all conditioned souls,  
Absorbed in the fight for reaching life’s eternal goal.  
Remembering who we are and what is our role.  
Yearning to be fixed as a part of the Whole.  
And someday, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we beg to join you again and personally assist you in pushing Kṛṣṇa consciousness with you in other planets.

Thank you so much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to engage in your service for one more year. We pray that you will continue to allow us to assist you in your preaching mission and eventually come to the platform of *ahaituky apratihatā*.

*We beg to remain you menial servants at the BBT, Los Angeles, California*.

## Bhaktivedanta Book Trust, North Europe

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

It is stated in the *śāstras* that there are two kinds of living entities: the *nitya-siddhas*, and the *nitya-baddhas*. The *nitya-baddha* living entities are described as being eternally conditioned within the material realm, always forgetful of their constitutional position as Kṛṣṇa’s eternal servants, taking repeated births and deaths, chewing the chewed life after life, and never being able to free themselves from material bondage. We, unfortunately, belong to that category of living entities.

The other category of living beings, the *nitya-siddhas*, are eternally liberated, perpetually Kṛṣṇa conscious souls. The *nitya-siddha* living entities are always conscious of their position as Kṛṣṇa’s eternal servants, and they are forever engaged in the blissful, loving service of the Lord. They are free from fear and anxiety, and they are full of compassion for the fallen souls. They never fall down, and they relish the ecstasy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness at every step. You Śrīla Prabhupāda, belong to that category of living entity.

Actually you are a resident of the spiritual world, but on the order of the Lord and out of your causeless mercy and compassion, you descended to this material world to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness on behalf of Lord Caitanya and the *guru-paramparā*. More specifically, you came to write books. Actually, your books are most wonderful and special. They are imbued with Kṛṣṇa-śakti, the potency to make anyone who reads them fully Kṛṣṇa conscious. Your books are the basis for a vast spiritual revolution in the hearts of men, which will change the course of history in this degraded age of Kali. Simply by the force of your transcendental books the whole world can be saved from imminent destruction.

You ordered your followers to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and you outlined the way to do it: by distribution of your transcendental books. What will our three minutes preaching do? What is our potency? What is the depth of our realization? A true follower of Your Divine Grace will take the straw in his mouth and humbly admit that actually you – in the form of your books – are the real Vaiṣṇava preacher, and thus dedicate his life to giving your books to the conditioned souls. If we read your books, follow the instructions given in them, thus gaining the potency to give them to others, we will without a doubt become Kṛṣṇa conscious within this lifetime, and go back home, back to Godhead, dragging thousands of conditioned souls along with us in the process.

This whole Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is simply based on your books. We at the NE BBT are trying to assist you in your mission of spreading your divine books far and wide by publishing them in German, French, Dutch, Danish, Norwegian, Icelandic, British, Swedish, Finnish, Estonian, Latvian, Lithuanian, Byelorussian, Ukranian, Russian, Polish, Czech, Slovak, Hungarian, Bulgarian, Romanian, Macedonian, Serbian, Croatian, Serbo-Croatian, Slovene, Armenian, Georgian, Azerbaijani, Turkmen, Kirgiz, Uzbek, Karakalpak, Tadjik, Tatar, Udmurt, Mongolian, Vietnamese and Chuvash so that the devotees in those countries can distribute them with great enthusiasm. So far we have only published the full set in a few of these languages, so the task ahead of us is enormous. We therefore want to take this opportunity at the auspicious occasion of your appearance within this material world, to pray for your mercy to accomplish the task that you have given us. Let us publish your books in all the languages of the world, so that fallen souls everywhere can get the opportunity to associate with Your Divine Grace, thus perfecting their lives by becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bless us that we may expand this mission of book production and book distribution unlimitedly. Allow us to please you by doing this.

*Your humble servants at the NE BBT production center at Korsnas Gard, Sweden*.

## Bhaktivedanta Institute

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All glories to Your Divine Grace! All glories to the most auspicious appearance day of Your Divine Grace!

Day by day your fame is increasing on the planet because more and more people are coming in touch with the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and sanctifying their lives. Thus you are flooding this world in an ever-increasing ocean of your transcendental pastimes. Thank you very much for allowing your followers in the Bhaktivedanta Institute to struggle and endeavor one more year in an effort to establish ourselves in the intellectual community. By your blessings, it looks like BI has been brought to a point where major expansion is imminent. By your constant inspiration and blessings, Henry Stapp, a well-known physicist traveled to Bombay and spent 40 days studying the Bhāgavata philosophy (Kapiladeva’s instructions to Mother Devahutī about the material nature) under the BI scholars. He wrote a scientific report on the outcome of his study that shows the relevance of Bhāgavata philosophy to modern physics. Bruce Mangan and three other scientists spent last summer working with our institute, thereby gaining some preliminary training in our process. Your Divine Grace said, “conduct conferences in major cities all over the world; I will send you men and money.” You gave us the method of preaching to scientists by referring us to Prabodhānanda Sarasvatī. Your Divine Grace explained thus in the morning walk which took place in Nairobi, Kenya, on October 28, 1975:

Prabhupāda: Wherever you go, you gather people and induce them by flattering them, by falling on their leg – some way or other, induce them. *Dante nidhāya tṛṇakaṁ padayor nipatya* (*Caitanya-candrāmṛta* 8.90). This is the process shown by Prabodānanda Sarasvatī, “Taking a straw in my mouth and falling down on your lotus feet.” *Kāku-śatam kṛtvā:* “And I am trying to please you by so many flattering words.” *Kṛtvā ahaṁ bravīmi:* “I have got some submission...” This is the process...

So now, when he will say, “All right, you can say what you want to say,” then *he sādhava:* “You are a great personality, nobleman.” He may be a loafer class \[laughter\], but give him all honor: “You are so great and so exalted,” *he sādhava*, “and so honest... My only submission is that whatever you have learned, you forget. Whatever nonsense you have learned...” Don’t say “nonsense.” \[laughter\] But we must know that he is a *pakkā* rascal, nonsense. \[laughs\] So don’t say directly “nonsense.” Say, “You are the great personality. So kindly, whatever you have learned, forget.”  
”Then what should I do?”

*He sādhavaḥ sakalam eve vihāya dūrāt:* “Kick out whatever nonsense you learned.”  
”Then what should I do?  
”Now, *caitanya-candra-caraṇe kurutānurāgam*: Kindly be submissive to Lord Caitanya, and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa.”  
This is our program. No argument. Because he is rascal, what is the use of arguing with him? He’s a rascal number one. You know that. You cannot expect any good argument from the rascals and fools. Where is the logic? Their logic is to beat them with shoes. That is the only logic. But that, if you do that, then you’ll not be able to preach...  
...Therefore, especially in this age, Caitanya Mahāprabhu says, “*tṛṇād api sunīcena taror iva sahiṣṇunā amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sada hariḥ*.” So we should preach like this, any rascal, because anyone who has no information of Kṛṣṇa, he’s a rascal. That’s all. That we must know. But if you say that – “You are rascal...” – then you cannot preach. If you directly call a rascal a rascal, he’ll be angry. *Mūrkhāyopadeśa hi prakopāya na śantaye...* In this way preaching is practical. Otherwise it is not possible. Everyone is thinking he is the most exalted personality, scientist, philosopher, and a great man. That is the material disease... You are dealing with all rascals. So if we call them directly \[rascal\], they will be angry. Your preaching will not be successful. So follow the principles enunciated by Prabodānanda Sarasvatī and Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

In this way Your Divine Grace emphasized long-term and lasting strategies for the BI preaching. As a result of the First International conference in San Francisco on the *Study of Consciousness Within Science* in 1990, and, as a result of the continuing monthly discussion group on *consciousness and science* in San Francisco, our activities are expanding in the Bay Area, and we see many prominent scientists becoming favorable and anxious to get connected with your Bhaktivedanta Institute.

In Bombay two well-qualified PhD’s are now ready to join full-time. Dr. Antarkar, a professor of Philosophy has started working on projects related to *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. BI Bombay will get affiliation with the Birla Institute of Technology and Science to grant M.S. degrees in consciousness studies with help from Dr. Venkateshvaran, the Director. We are also approaching the government of India to gain 100% tax-exemption for scientific research conducted at the Institute. We realize we ourselves are tiny and incapable of doing much. But Your Divine Grace has the perfect vision. You are guiding us and giving us a great opportunity. We are humbly trying to fulfill your desires to establish this institute as the preaching arm of this ISKCON society. Your Divine Grace said, “B.I. is meant for boosting the prestige of ISKCON.” There is much more to be accomplished, but Your Divine Grace is compassionately guiding us in our efforts. You are already sending qualified men with the right attitude, and you are pointing out the resources to help serve you better. We pray for your blessings and guidance more and more in the coming months as we are preparing for the *conference of the century* in 1996 in Calcutta – The Second World Congress for the Synthesis of Science and Religion – as part of the ISKCON celebrations of Your Transcendental Appearance Centennial. Oh, when will that day come when scientists and intellectual leaders of the world will recognize Your Divine Grace as the real scientist, as the man who changed the course of this trouble-ridden world in Kali-yuga, and thus when will they follow in your footsteps?

*Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare*  
*Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare*

Pleading for your mercy,  
Your servants in the Bhaktivedanta Institute, Bombay and Oakland.

## Bhaktivedanta Union

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

We feel unqualified to glorify you, so we shall take help from that great *brāhmaṇa,* Cāṇakya Paṇḍita, who has so beautifully described some of your invincible virtues.

One excellent thing that should be learned from a lion is that whatever a man intends to do should be done by him with a wholehearted and strenuous effort. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, mercilessly tore the *māyāvādīs* and atheists to pieces).

The wise man should restrain his senses like the crane and accomplish hi purpose with due knowledge of his place, time and ability. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, among the *svāmīs* coming over from India, did not become a victim to the West’s wealth and women but rather engaged in Kṛṣṇa’s service).

To wake at the proper time, to make a bold stand and fight, to make a fair division of property among relations and to earn one’s bread by personal exertion are the four excellent things to be learned from a cock. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, rose at 1:00 AM, assaulted the rascal scientists, bequeathed the world of preaching to your faithful disciples, and outworked all of your disciples combined).

Union in privacy with one’s wife, boldness, storing away useful items, watchfulness and not easily trusting others are the five things to be learned from the crow. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, showed a perfect example of *gṛhastha* life, journeyed across the sea at seventy, revealed rare secret Purāṇic gems, noticed the frustration in the faces of the sense-gratifiers, and didn’t care a fig for the “Uptown swamis” who advised you to adopt Western habits).

Contentment with little or nothing to eat, although one may have a great appetite, to wake instantly although one may be in deep slumber, unflinching devotion to the master and bravery. These six qualities should be learned from the dog. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, nibbled, fasted or feasted as the Lord dictated, remained awake even during slumber, lived and breathed Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s order, and singly challenged Māyā’s vast army).

Although an ass is tired, he continues to carry his burden, he is unmindful of cold and heat and he is always contented, these three things should be learned from an ass. (Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, translated till the very end, preached with equal happiness in New York’s snow and India’s sun, and never complained).

Please bless us with your causeless mercy to spread the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa.

*Bhaktivedanta Union-KrishnaFest, Tampa, Florida*.  
(Written by Dānavīra dāsa Vānacārī)

## Food for Life

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

For some of us the thought of you working in the kitchen of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī assisting Her in Her pastimes of preparing Lord Kṛṣṇa’s lunch is very appealing. After all, cooking and dancing are Śrīmatī Rādhikā’s super-excellent skills, and the services

She likes to render the most. Your father, Gour Mohan De prayed that you should one day become a pure devotee of Rādhārāṇī and you fulfilled his wishes by introducing *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana* and *prasādam* distribution to the fallen souls of this world.

We can just imagine you assisting in this capacity, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You were very fond of cooking.
